### FILE: src/humboldt/database.py
```text
0001 | """Versioned SQLite storage for immutable, reproducible edition snapshots.
0002 | 
0003 | The packaged SQL is the identifier allowlist and schema authority. Sealing uses
0004 | ordinary SQLite triggers, not a security boundary against a database owner who
0005 | can remove schema objects. Readers should also use a read-only connection.
0006 | """
0007 | 
0008 | from collections.abc import Mapping
0009 | from contextlib import contextmanager
0010 | from datetime import datetime, timezone
0011 | from functools import lru_cache
0012 | import hashlib
0013 | import heapq
0014 | from importlib import resources
0015 | import itertools
0016 | import json
0017 | from pathlib import Path
0018 | import re
0019 | import sqlite3
0020 | 
0021 | 
0022 | class SchemaError(ValueError):
0023 |     """A database or migration history does not match the packaged schema."""
0024 | 
0025 | 
0026 | class SealedError(SchemaError):
0027 |     """A construction operation was attempted on a sealed snapshot."""
0028 | 
0029 | 
0030 | _HISTORY_SQL = """CREATE TABLE schema_migration (
0031 |     version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL CHECK (version > 0),
0032 |     name TEXT NOT NULL,
0033 |     sha256 TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(sha256) = 64),
0034 |     applied_at TEXT NOT NULL
0035 | )"""
0036 | _SEAL_PREFIX = "humboldt_sealed_"
0037 | _SAVEPOINTS = itertools.count()
0038 | 
0039 | 
0040 | def connect(path=":memory:", *, readonly=False):
0041 |     """Return a Row connection with foreign keys and recursive triggers enabled."""
0042 |     if readonly:
0043 |         if str(path) == ":memory:":
0044 |             raise ValueError("a read-only connection requires an existing file")
0045 |         uri = Path(path).expanduser().resolve().as_uri() + "?mode=ro"
0046 |         db = sqlite3.connect(uri, uri=True)
0047 |     else:
0048 |         filename = ":memory:" if str(path) == ":memory:" else str(Path(path).expanduser())
0049 |         db = sqlite3.connect(filename)
0050 |     db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
0051 |     db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
0052 |     db.execute("PRAGMA recursive_triggers = ON")
0053 |     return db
0054 | 
0055 | 
0056 | def _identifier(name):
0057 |     # Callers supply introspected names, never unchecked input identifiers.
0058 |     return '"' + name.replace('"', '""') + '"'
0059 | 
0060 | 
0061 | def _migrations():
0062 |     directory = resources.files(__package__).joinpath("migrations")
0063 |     found = []
0064 |     for path in directory.iterdir():
0065 |         if not path.name.endswith(".sql"):
0066 |             continue
0067 |         match = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+)-[a-z0-9-]+\.sql", path.name)
0068 |         if not match:
0069 |             raise SchemaError(f"invalid packaged migration name: {path.name}")
0070 |         raw = path.read_bytes()
0071 |         found.append((int(match[1]), path.name, raw.decode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()))
0072 |     found.sort()
0073 |     if not found or [x[0] for x in found] != list(range(1, len(found) + 1)):
0074 |         raise SchemaError("packaged migrations must be consecutively numbered from 1")
0075 |     return tuple(found)
0076 | 
0077 | 
0078 | def _execute_script(db, script):
0079 |     # executescript commits an existing transaction. Execute complete statements
0080 |     # individually so DDL, history and PRAGMA user_version share one savepoint.
0081 |     pending = ""
0082 |     for line in script.splitlines(keepends=True):
0083 |         pending += line
0084 |         if sqlite3.complete_statement(pending):
0085 |             db.execute(pending)
0086 |             pending = ""
0087 |     if pending.strip():
0088 |         raise SchemaError("incomplete packaged SQL statement")
0089 | 
0090 | 
0091 | @contextmanager
0092 | def _atomic(db):
0093 |     name = f"humboldt_operation_{next(_SAVEPOINTS)}"
0094 |     db.execute(f"SAVEPOINT {name}")
0095 |     try:
0096 |         yield
0097 |         db.execute(f"RELEASE SAVEPOINT {name}")
0098 |     except BaseException:
0099 |         db.execute(f"ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT {name}")
0100 |         db.execute(f"RELEASE SAVEPOINT {name}")
0101 |         raise
0102 | 
0103 | 
0104 | def _objects(db):
0105 |     return {
0106 |         (row[0], row[1]): row[2]
0107 |         for row in db.execute(
0108 |             "SELECT type, name, sql FROM main.sqlite_master "
0109 |             "WHERE sql IS NOT NULL AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'"
0110 |         )
0111 |     }
0112 | 
0113 | 
0114 | def _catalog(db):
0115 |     result = {}
0116 |     for kind, name in _objects(db):
0117 |         if kind != "table":
0118 |             continue
0119 |         columns = tuple(row[1] for row in db.execute(f"PRAGMA main.table_info({_identifier(name)})"))
0120 |         parents = frozenset(row[2] for row in db.execute(f"PRAGMA main.foreign_key_list({_identifier(name)})"))
0121 |         result[name] = (columns, parents)
0122 |     return result
0123 | 
0124 | 
0125 | @lru_cache(maxsize=8)
0126 | def _expected(version, migrations):
0127 |     db = sqlite3.connect(":memory:")
0128 |     try:
0129 |         db.execute(_HISTORY_SQL)
0130 |         for number, _name, script, _checksum in migrations:
0131 |             if number <= version:
0132 |                 _execute_script(db, script)
0133 |         return _objects(db), _catalog(db)
0134 |     finally:
0135 |         db.close()
0136 | 
0137 | 
0138 | def _sealed_sql(table, operation):
0139 |     name = _SEAL_PREFIX + table + "_" + operation.lower()
0140 |     sql = (
0141 |         f"CREATE TRIGGER {_identifier(name)} BEFORE {operation} ON {_identifier(table)} "
0142 |         "BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'database is sealed; build a fresh snapshot'); END"
0143 |     )
0144 |     return name, sql
0145 | 
0146 | 
0147 | def _is_sealed(db):
0148 |     # Detect the physical protections even if the metadata was damaged.
0149 |     return db.execute(
0150 |         "SELECT 1 FROM main.sqlite_master WHERE type = 'trigger' "
0151 |         "AND substr(name, 1, ?) = ? LIMIT 1", (len(_SEAL_PREFIX), _SEAL_PREFIX)
0152 |     ).fetchone() is not None
0153 | 
0154 | 
0155 | def _history(db, migrations):
0156 |     version = db.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0]
0157 |     if version > len(migrations):
0158 |         raise SchemaError(f"unknown future schema version {version}; supported through {len(migrations)}")
0159 |     objects = _objects(db)
0160 |     if ("table", "schema_migration") not in objects:
0161 |         if objects or version:
0162 |             raise SchemaError("nonempty unversioned database; import its records into a fresh database")
0163 |         return 0, False
0164 |     actual_columns = _catalog(db)["schema_migration"][0]
0165 |     if actual_columns != ("version", "name", "sha256", "applied_at"):
0166 |         raise SchemaError("unknown schema_migration columns")
0167 |     rows = list(db.execute("SELECT version, name, sha256 FROM main.schema_migration ORDER BY version"))
0168 |     recorded = [row[0] for row in rows]
0169 |     if recorded != list(range(1, len(rows) + 1)) or len(rows) != version:
0170 |         raise SchemaError("schema version and consecutive migration history disagree")
0171 |     for number, name, checksum in rows:
0172 |         if number > len(migrations):
0173 |             raise SchemaError(f"unknown future migration {number}")
0174 |         expected = migrations[number - 1]
0175 |         if name != expected[1] or checksum != expected[3]:
0176 |             raise SchemaError(f"migration {number} name/checksum mismatch")
0177 |     return version, True
0178 | 
0179 | 
0180 | def _assert_schema(db, version, migrations):
0181 |     expected, catalog = _expected(version, migrations)
0182 |     expected = dict(expected)
0183 |     if _is_sealed(db):
0184 |         if version < 2:
0185 |             raise SchemaError("sealing requires infrastructure schema version 2")
0186 |         for table in catalog:
0187 |             for operation in ("INSERT", "UPDATE", "DELETE"):
0188 |                 name, sql = _sealed_sql(table, operation)
0189 |                 expected[("trigger", name)] = sql
0190 |     actual = _objects(db)
0191 |     if set(actual) != set(expected):
0192 |         missing = sorted(set(expected) - set(actual))
0193 |         extra = sorted(set(actual) - set(expected))
0194 |         raise SchemaError(f"unknown or incomplete schema objects; missing={missing}, extra={extra}")
0195 |     for key in expected:
0196 |         # SQLite preserves submitted DDL except for its terminal semicolon.
0197 |         if actual[key].strip().rstrip(";") != expected[key].strip().rstrip(";"):
0198 |             raise SchemaError(f"schema definition mismatch: {key[0]} {key[1]}")
0199 |     if _is_sealed(db):
0200 |         row = db.execute("SELECT value_json FROM main.build_metadata WHERE key = 'sealed'").fetchone()
0201 |         if row is None or row[0] != "true":
0202 |             raise SchemaError("sealed triggers and build metadata disagree")
0203 |     return catalog
0204 | 
0205 | 
0206 | def _state(db):
0207 |     migrations = _migrations()
0208 |     version, present = _history(db, migrations)
0209 |     if not present:
0210 |         raise SchemaError("database has not been migrated")
0211 |     return version, _assert_schema(db, version, migrations)
0212 | 
0213 | 
0214 | def migrate(db, target=None):
0215 |     """Apply packaged checksummed migrations atomically; never downgrade/adopt.
0216 | 
0217 |     Existing legacy snapshots are input evidence, not migration targets. Import
0218 |     their exported rows into a fresh versioned database instead.
0219 |     """
0220 |     if not db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys").fetchone()[0]:
0221 |         raise SchemaError("foreign keys must be enabled before migration")
0222 |     migrations = _migrations()
0223 |     latest = len(migrations)
0224 |     if target is None:
0225 |         target = latest
0226 |     if type(target) is not int or not 0 <= target <= latest:
0227 |         raise SchemaError(f"unknown target schema version {target!r}; supported through {latest}")
0228 |     current, present = _history(db, migrations)
0229 |     if _is_sealed(db):
0230 |         raise SealedError("database is sealed; migrate/rebuild a fresh unsealed snapshot")
0231 |     if present:
0232 |         _assert_schema(db, current, migrations)
0233 |     if target < current:
0234 |         raise SchemaError(f"schema downgrade from {current} to {target} is not supported")
0235 |     with _atomic(db):
0236 |         if not present:
0237 |             db.execute(_HISTORY_SQL)
0238 |         for number, name, script, checksum in migrations:
0239 |             if current < number <= target:
0240 |                 _execute_script(db, script)
0241 |                 db.execute(
0242 |                     "INSERT INTO main.schema_migration(version, name, sha256, applied_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
0243 |                     (number, name, checksum, datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),
0244 |                 )
0245 |                 db.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version = {number}")
0246 |         _assert_schema(db, target, migrations)
0247 |     return target
0248 | 
0249 | 
0250 | def seal(db):
0251 |     """Block direct row writes to every current table, including metadata/history."""
0252 |     version, catalog = _state(db)
0253 |     if version < 2:
0254 |         raise SchemaError("migrate to version 2 before sealing")
0255 |     if _is_sealed(db):
0256 |         return
0257 |     errors = db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").fetchall()
0258 |     integrity = [row[0] for row in db.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check")]
0259 |     if errors or integrity != ["ok"]:
0260 |         raise SchemaError("cannot seal a database with integrity or foreign-key errors")
0261 |     with _atomic(db):
0262 |         db.execute("INSERT INTO main.build_metadata(key, value_json) VALUES ('sealed', 'true')")
0263 |         for table in sorted(catalog):
0264 |             for operation in ("INSERT", "UPDATE", "DELETE"):
0265 |                 _name, sql = _sealed_sql(table, operation)
0266 |                 db.execute(sql)
0267 |         _assert_schema(db, version, _migrations())
0268 | 
0269 | 
0270 | def report(db):
0271 |     """Return JSON-serializable counts and physical integrity diagnostics."""
0272 |     version, catalog = _state(db)
0273 |     integrity = [row[0] for row in db.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check")]
0274 |     return {
0275 |         "schema_version": version,
0276 |         "sealed": _is_sealed(db),
0277 |         "counts": {name: db.execute(f"SELECT count(*) FROM main.{_identifier(name)}").fetchone()[0]
0278 |                    for name in sorted(catalog)},
0279 |         "integrity": "ok" if integrity == ["ok"] else "; ".join(integrity),
0280 |         "foreign_key_errors": [dict(zip(("table", "rowid", "parent", "fkid"), tuple(row)))
0281 |                                for row in db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check")],
0282 |     }
0283 | 
0284 | 
0285 | def _json(value):
0286 |     return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
0287 | 
0288 | 
0289 | def logical_digest(db):
0290 |     """Hash durable rows canonically, excluding transient build metadata/time.
0291 | 
0292 |     Recorded revision timestamps are durable provenance and remain included.
0293 |     build_metadata (including its stored digest/sealed flag) and migration
0294 |     applied_at are excluded. Physical row order, file layout and path do not
0295 |     contribute. Resource manifests remain verified separately by ingest.
0296 |     """
0297 |     _version, catalog = _state(db)
0298 |     digest = hashlib.sha256()
0299 |     for table in sorted(catalog):
0300 |         if table == "build_metadata":
0301 |             continue
0302 |         columns = tuple(c for c in catalog[table][0]
0303 |                         if not (table == "schema_migration" and c == "applied_at"))
0304 |         digest.update((_json([table, columns]) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
0305 |         query = ", ".join(_identifier(c) for c in columns)
0306 |         rows = []
0307 |         for row in db.execute(f"SELECT {query} FROM main.{_identifier(table)}"):
0308 |             values = [{"sqlite_blob_hex": value.hex()} if isinstance(value, bytes) else value for value in row]
0309 |             rows.append(_json(values))
0310 |         for row in sorted(rows):
0311 |             digest.update((row + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
0312 |     return digest.hexdigest()
0313 | 
0314 | 
0315 | def _table_order(names, catalog):
0316 |     remaining = {name: set(catalog[name][1]) & set(names) - {name} for name in names}
0317 |     ordered = []
0318 |     while remaining:
0319 |         ready = sorted(name for name, parents in remaining.items() if not parents)
0320 |         if not ready:
0321 |             # Deferred foreign keys support a future genuine inter-table cycle;
0322 |             # the final FK check still rejects an incomplete import.
0323 |             ordered.extend(sorted(remaining))
0324 |             break
0325 |         ordered.extend(ready)
0326 |         for name in ready:
0327 |             remaining.pop(name)
0328 |         for parents in remaining.values():
0329 |             parents.difference_update(ready)
0330 |     return ordered
0331 | 
0332 | 
0333 | def _unit_order(rows):
0334 |     by_id = {}
0335 |     for row in rows:
0336 |         unit_id = row.get("id")
0337 |         if not isinstance(unit_id, str) or unit_id in by_id:
0338 |             raise ValueError("unit records require unique explicit string IDs")
0339 |         by_id[unit_id] = row
0340 |     children = {}
0341 |     ready = []
0342 |     for unit_id, row in by_id.items():
0343 |         parent = row.get("parent_id")
0344 |         if parent in by_id:
0345 |             children.setdefault(parent, []).append(unit_id)
0346 |         else:
0347 |             heapq.heappush(ready, unit_id)
0348 |     result = []
0349 |     while ready:
0350 |         unit_id = heapq.heappop(ready)
0351 |         result.append(by_id[unit_id])
0352 |         for child in children.get(unit_id, ()):
0353 |             heapq.heappush(ready, child)
0354 |     if len(result) != len(rows):
0355 |         raise ValueError("unit parent cycle in imported records")
0356 |     return result
0357 | 
0358 | 
0359 | def insert_records(db, tables):
0360 |     """Atomically import allowlisted rows, preserving IDs and legacy constraints."""
0361 |     _version, catalog = _state(db)
0362 |     if _is_sealed(db):
0363 |         raise SealedError("database is sealed; import into a fresh snapshot")
0364 |     if not db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys").fetchone()[0]:
0365 |         raise SchemaError("foreign keys must be enabled before record import")
0366 |     if not isinstance(tables, Mapping):
0367 |         raise ValueError("tables must be a mapping of table names to record lists")
0368 |     supplied = {}
0369 |     for table, rows in tables.items():
0370 |         if table not in catalog or table == "schema_migration":
0371 |             raise ValueError(f"unknown or managed record table: {table!r}")
0372 |         if not isinstance(rows, (list, tuple)):
0373 |             raise ValueError(f"records for {table} must be a list")
0374 |         supplied[table] = []
0375 |         for row in rows:
0376 |             if not isinstance(row, Mapping) or not row:
0377 |                 raise ValueError(f"records for {table} must be nonempty mappings")
0378 |             unknown = set(row) - set(catalog[table][0])
0379 |             if unknown:
0380 |                 raise ValueError(f"unknown columns for {table}: {sorted(map(str, unknown))}")
0381 |             if table == "build_metadata" and row.get("key") == "sealed":
0382 |                 raise ValueError("build_metadata.sealed is reserved for seal()")
0383 |             if table == "alignment" and row.get("status", "draft") not in ("draft", "proposed"):
0384 |                 raise ValueError("alignment status must be draft or proposed")
0385 |             supplied[table].append(dict(row))
0386 |     if "unit" in supplied:
0387 |         supplied["unit"] = _unit_order(supplied["unit"])
0388 |     deferred = db.execute("PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys").fetchone()[0]
0389 |     try:
0390 |         with _atomic(db):
0391 |             db.execute("PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys = ON")
0392 |             proposed = []
0393 |             for table in _table_order(supplied, catalog):
0394 |                 for original in supplied[table]:
0395 |                     row = dict(original)
0396 |                     if table == "alignment" and row.get("status") == "proposed":
0397 |                         proposed.append(row["id"])
0398 |                         row["status"] = "draft"
0399 |                     columns = [c for c in catalog[table][0] if c in row]
0400 |                     sql = (f"INSERT INTO main.{_identifier(table)} ("
0401 |                            + ", ".join(_identifier(c) for c in columns)
0402 |                            + ") VALUES (" + ", ".join("?" for _ in columns) + ")")
0403 |                     db.execute(sql, tuple(row[c] for c in columns))
0404 |             for alignment_id in proposed:
0405 |                 db.execute("UPDATE main.alignment SET status = 'proposed' WHERE id = ?", (alignment_id,))
0406 |             errors = db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").fetchall()
0407 |             if errors:
0408 |                 raise sqlite3.IntegrityError(f"foreign-key errors after record import: {[tuple(x) for x in errors[:5]]}")
0409 |     finally:
0410 |         db.execute(f"PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys = {int(deferred)}")
```

```text
0179 | def _build(project,output,*,changes=True,force=False):
0180 |     root=project_root(project);output=Path(output).resolve();output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
0181 |     # Never permit a typo to overwrite authoritative inputs, code or historical artifacts.
0182 |     if output==root or output.is_relative_to(root) and output.parts[len(root.parts)] not in {'build'}:raise ValueError('project build output must be under build/; external fresh output paths are also allowed')
0183 |     if output.exists() and not force:raise ValueError('output exists; choose a fresh path or use --force for build/ output')
0184 |     if output.exists() and not output.is_relative_to(root/'build'):raise ValueError('force replacement is confined to project build/')
0185 |     fd,temp=tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='.'+output.name+'.',suffix='.sqlite',dir=output.parent);os.close(fd);temporary=Path(temp)
0186 |     db=database.connect(temporary)
0187 |     try:

0189 |         digest=database.logical_digest(db)
0190 |         db.execute('INSERT INTO build_metadata(key,value_json) VALUES (?,?)',('logical_digest',canonical(digest)));db.commit()
0191 |         database.seal(db);db.commit();report=database.report(db)
0192 |         db.close();db=None
0193 |         if output.exists() and not force:raise ValueError('output appeared during build; refusing replacement')
0194 |         if force:os.replace(temporary,output)
0195 |         else:os.link(temporary,output);temporary.unlink()
0196 |         sync_directory(output.parent)
0197 |         return dict(report,logical_digest=digest,output=str(output))
0198 |     finally:
0199 |         if db is not None:db.close()

0223 |         if safe_id!=bundle['id']:raise ValueError('passage dataset ID must use letters/numbers/_/-')

0227 |         manifest['datasets'].append(dict(path=relative,sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()))
0228 |         candidate=database.connect()

0230 |         finally:candidate.close()
0231 |         # Publish only after validation. An interruption between these two
0232 |         # publications can leave an unreferenced file, never an invalid manifest.
0233 |         atomic_json(dest,bundle,replace=False)
0234 |         atomic_json(manifest_path,manifest)
```

### FILE: src/humboldt/queries.py
```text
0001 | """Read-only, JSON-safe interrogation of an edition database.
0002 | 
0003 | Span overlap is evidence of context, not proof that an annotation or translation
0004 | is an exact semantic equivalent. Public results keep that distinction explicit.
0005 | """
0006 | from __future__ import annotations
0007 | 
0008 | import json
0009 | import re
0010 | from collections import defaultdict
0011 | 
0012 | 
0013 | class NotFound(LookupError):
0014 |     """The requested durable identifier is absent from this database."""
0015 | 
0016 | 
0017 | KINDS = {
0018 |     "lexeme": "lexeme", "term": "lexeme", "sense": "term_sense",
0019 |     "term_sense": "term_sense", "term_use": "term_use",
0020 |     "attestation": "sense_attestation", "sense_attestation": "sense_attestation",
0021 |     "evidence": "evidence", "decision": "decision", "unit": "unit",
0022 |     "text_version": "text_version", "version": "text_version",
0023 |     "analysis": "analysis", "alignment": "alignment", "term_entry": "term_entry",
0024 |     "composite_attestation": "composite_attestation",
0025 |     "revision": "revision_history", "revision_history": "revision_history",
0026 | }
0027 | 
0028 | 
0029 | def _rows(db, sql, parameters=()):
0030 |     cursor = db.execute(sql, parameters)
0031 |     names = [column[0] for column in cursor.description]
0032 |     return [dict(zip(names, row)) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
0033 | 
0034 | 
0035 | def _one(db, sql, parameters=()):
0036 |     rows = _rows(db, sql, parameters)
0037 |     return rows[0] if rows else None
0038 | 
0039 | 
0040 | def _has(db, table):
0041 |     return bool(db.execute("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE name=? AND type IN ('table','view')", (table,)).fetchone())
0042 | 
0043 | 
0044 | def _json(value, default=None):
0045 |     if value is None:
0046 |         return default
0047 |     try:
0048 |         return json.loads(value)
0049 |     except (ValueError, TypeError):
0050 |         return default
0051 | 
0052 | 
0053 | def _by_ids(db, table, column, values):
0054 |     # Only internal, fixed table/column names reach this helper.
0055 |     values = list(dict.fromkeys(values))
0056 |     if not values or not _has(db, table):
0057 |         return []
0058 |     result = []
0059 |     for offset in range(0, len(values), 200):
0060 |         group = values[offset:offset + 200]
0061 |         result.extend(_rows(db, f'SELECT * FROM "{table}" WHERE "{column}" IN ({",".join("?" for _ in group)})', group))
0062 |     return result
0063 | 
0064 | 
0065 | def _unit(db, unit_id):
0066 |     return _one(db, "SELECT u.*, substr(v.body,u.start+1,u.end-u.start) AS text, v.language, v.title AS version_title FROM unit u JOIN text_version v ON v.id=u.version_id WHERE u.id=?", (unit_id,))
0067 | 
0068 | 
0069 | def _spatial(selected, annotation):
0070 |     if selected["version_id"] != annotation["version_id"]:
0071 |         return "linked_context"
0072 |     a, b = selected["start"], selected["end"]
0073 |     c, d = annotation["start"], annotation["end"]
0074 |     if a == c and b == d:
0075 |         return "exact_span"
0076 |     if c <= a and d >= b:
0077 |         return "containing_context"
0078 |     if a <= c and b >= d:
0079 |         return "contained_annotation"
0080 |     return "overlapping_context"
0081 | 
0082 | 
0083 | def _annotations(db, unit):
0084 |     links = []
0085 |     for table, column in (("unit_decision", "unit_id"), ("unit_evidence", "unit_id"), ("alignment_member", "unit_id"), ("term_occurrence", "unit_id"), ("term_expression_part", "unit_id")):
0086 |         if _has(db, table):
0087 |             links.append(f'EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM {table} z WHERE z.{column}=u.id)')
0088 |     if _has(db, "sense_attestation"):
0089 |         links.append("EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sense_attestation z WHERE u.id IN (z.checked_context_unit,z.checked_expression_unit,z.ocr_context_unit,z.source_page_unit))")
0090 |     keep = "u.kind NOT IN ('word','written_word_candidate')"
0091 |     if links:
0092 |         keep += " OR " + " OR ".join(links)
0093 |     return _rows(db, f"SELECT u.*, substr(v.body,u.start+1,u.end-u.start) AS text, v.language, v.title AS version_title FROM unit u JOIN text_version v ON v.id=u.version_id WHERE u.version_id=? AND u.start<? AND u.end>? AND u.id<>? AND ({keep}) ORDER BY u.start,u.end,u.id", (unit["version_id"], unit["end"], unit["start"], unit["id"]))
0094 | 
0095 | 
0096 | def _evidence(db, evidence_id):
0097 |     row = _one(db, "SELECT * FROM evidence WHERE id=?", (evidence_id,)) if _has(db, "evidence") else None
0098 |     if row and _has(db, "evidence_detail"):
0099 |         detail = _one(db, "SELECT metadata_json FROM evidence_detail WHERE evidence_id=?", (evidence_id,))
0100 |         row["detail"] = _json(detail["metadata_json"], {}) if detail else {}
0101 |     return row

0103 | 
0104 | def _history(db, targets, kind=None):
0105 |     if not _has(db, "revision_history"):
0106 |         return {"available": False, "revisions": [], "impacts": [], "note": "This snapshot has no revision-history tables; this does not establish that no earlier revision occurred."}
0107 |     revisions = _by_ids(db, "revision_history", "target_id", targets)
0108 |     impacts = _by_ids(db, "change_impact", "entity_id", targets)
0109 |     if kind:
0110 |         revisions = [r for r in revisions if r.get("kind") == kind]
0111 |         impacts = [r for r in impacts if r.get("entity_kind") == kind]
0112 |     revisions += _by_ids(db, "revision_history", "id", [r["revision_id"] for r in impacts])
0113 |     unique = {r["id"]: r for r in revisions}
0114 |     result = []
0115 |     for row in sorted(unique.values(), key=lambda r: (r.get("recorded_at", ""), r["id"])):
0116 |         row = dict(row)
0117 |         for key in ("old_value", "new_value", "evidence", "metadata"):
0118 |             row[key] = _json(row.get(key + "_json"), None)
0119 |         result.append(row)
0120 |     return {"available": True, "revisions": result, "impacts": impacts}
0121 | 
0122 | 
0123 | def _sense(db, row):
0124 |     result = dict(row)
0125 |     result["renderings"] = _rows(db, "SELECT rank,rendering FROM sense_rendering WHERE sense_id=? ORDER BY rank", (row["id"],)) if _has(db, "sense_rendering") else []
0126 |     result["uses"] = _by_ids(db, "term_use", "sense_id", [row["id"]])
0127 |     result["attestations"] = _by_ids(db, "sense_attestation", "sense_id", [row["id"]])
0128 |     for attestation in result["attestations"]:
0129 |         attestation["evidence"] = _evidence(db, attestation["evidence_id"])
0130 |         attestation["expression_parts"] = _by_ids(db, "term_expression_part", "attestation_id", [attestation["id"]])
0131 |         attestation["composite_memberships"] = _by_ids(db, "composite_member", "attestation_id", [attestation["id"]])
0132 |     result["composites"] = _by_ids(db, "composite_attestation", "sense_id", [row["id"]])
0133 |     for composite in result["composites"]:
0134 |         composite["members"] = _by_ids(db, "composite_member", "group_id", [composite["id"]])
0135 |     return result

0137 | 
0138 | def entity(db, kind, id):
0139 |     """Retrieve an allowlisted entity. ``record`` is the unmodified SQL row.
0140 | 
0141 |     Keeping the raw row separate permits conservative revision fingerprints;
0142 |     decoded display metadata and history must never enter that fingerprint.
0143 |     """
0144 |     table = KINDS.get(kind)
0145 |     if not table:
0146 |         raise ValueError("Unknown entity kind: " + str(kind))
0147 |     if not _has(db, table):
0148 |         raise NotFound(f"Entity kind {table} is unavailable in this snapshot")
0149 |     row = _one(db, f'SELECT * FROM "{table}" WHERE id=?', (str(id),))
0150 |     if not row:
0151 |         raise NotFound(f"No {table} with identifier {id}")
0152 |     out = {"kind": table, "record": row}
0153 |     targets = [row["id"]]
0154 |     if table == "unit":
0155 |         out["trace"] = trace(db, id)
0156 |     elif table == "lexeme":
0157 |         out["forms"] = _by_ids(db, "lexeme_form", "lexeme_id", [id])
0158 |         out["senses"] = [_sense(db, s) for s in _by_ids(db, "term_sense", "lexeme_id", [id])]
0159 |         out["relations"] = _by_ids(db, "term_relation", "lexeme_id", [id])
0160 |         out["controls"] = []
0161 |         for link in _by_ids(db, "lexeme_control", "lexeme_id", [id]):
0162 |             control = _one(db, "SELECT * FROM terminology_control WHERE id=?", (link["control_id"],))
0163 |             out["controls"].append({**link, "control": _json(control["metadata_json"], {}) if control else None})
0164 |         out["domains"] = _json(row.get("domains_json"), [])
0165 |         out["roles"] = _json(row.get("roles_json"), [])
0166 |         targets += [s["id"] for s in out["senses"]]
0167 |     elif table == "term_sense":
0168 |         out["sense"] = _sense(db, row)
0169 |     elif table == "decision":
0170 |         out["evidence"] = [_evidence(db, r["evidence_id"]) for r in _by_ids(db, "decision_evidence", "decision_id", [id])]
0171 |         out["units"] = _by_ids(db, "unit_decision", "decision_id", [id])
0172 |     elif table == "evidence":
0173 |         out["evidence"] = _evidence(db, id)
0174 |     elif table == "text_version":
0175 |         out["analyses"] = _by_ids(db, "analysis", "version_id", [id])
0176 |         out["blocks"] = _blocks(db, id)
0177 |     elif table == "alignment":
0178 |         out["members"] = _alignment_members(db, id)
0179 |     elif table == "composite_attestation":
0180 |         out["members"] = _by_ids(db, "composite_member", "group_id", [id])
0181 |     elif table == "term_entry":
0182 |         out["forms"] = _json(row.get("forms_json"), [])
0183 |         out["candidates"] = _json(row.get("candidates_json"), [])
0184 |         out["evidence"] = [_evidence(db, r["evidence_id"]) for r in _by_ids(db, "term_entry_evidence", "entry_id", [id])]
0185 |         out["families"] = _by_ids(db, "term_entry_family", "entry_id", [id])
0186 |     out["history"] = _history(db, targets, None if table == "lexeme" else table)
0187 |     return out
0188 |

0190 | def _alignment_members(db, alignment_id):
0191 |     rows = _by_ids(db, "alignment_member", "alignment_id", [alignment_id])
0192 |     return [{**r, "unit": _unit(db, r["unit_id"])} for r in rows]

0195 | def _alignments(db, unit_ids):
0196 |     links = _by_ids(db, "alignment_member", "unit_id", unit_ids)
0197 |     records = _by_ids(db, "alignment", "id", [r["alignment_id"] for r in links])
0198 |     return [{**r, "members": _alignment_members(db, r["id"])} for r in sorted(records, key=lambda r: r["id"])]

0200 | 
0201 | def trace(db, unit_id):
0202 |     """Trace a span through ancestry, overlapping annotations and alignments.
0203 | 
0204 |     Sibling annotations are deliberately searched by interval. In particular,
0205 |     a written word need not be the child of a term annotation to expose that
0206 |     annotation's decision. Alignment-derived links always remain contextual.
0207 |     """
0208 |     selected = _unit(db, str(unit_id))
0209 |     if selected is None:
0210 |         raise NotFound("No unit with identifier " + str(unit_id))
0211 |     ancestors, seen, parent = [], {selected["id"]}, selected.get("parent_id")
0212 |     while parent:
0213 |         if parent in seen:
0214 |             raise ValueError("Cyclic unit ancestry at " + parent)
0215 |         seen.add(parent)
0216 |         row = _unit(db, parent)
0217 |         if row is None:
0218 |             raise ValueError("Missing parent unit " + parent)
0219 |         ancestors.append(row)
0220 |         parent = row.get("parent_id")
0221 |     annotations = _annotations(db, selected)
0222 |     roles = {selected["id"]: {"unit_id": selected["id"], "scope": "exact", "relation": "selected", "via": "selected"}}
0223 |     for row in ancestors + annotations:
0224 |         relation = _spatial(selected, row)
0225 |         roles.setdefault(row["id"], {"unit_id": row["id"], "scope": "exact" if relation == "exact_span" else "context", "relation": relation, "via": "overlap_or_ancestry"})
0226 |     first_alignments = _alignments(db, roles)
0227 |     counterpart_rows = {}
0228 |     for alignment in first_alignments:
0229 |         for member in alignment["members"]:
0230 |             row = member["unit"]
0231 |             if row and row["version_id"] != selected["version_id"]:
0232 |                 counterpart_rows[row["id"]] = row
0233 |                 for context in [row] + _annotations(db, row):
0234 |                     roles.setdefault(context["id"], {"unit_id": context["id"], "scope": "context", "relation": "aligned_context", "via": alignment["id"]})
0235 |     alignments = _alignments(db, roles)
0236 |     decision_links = _by_ids(db, "unit_decision", "unit_id", roles)
0237 |     evidence_links = _by_ids(db, "unit_evidence", "unit_id", roles)
0238 |     terms = []
0239 |     attestations = {}
0240 |     for column in ("checked_context_unit", "checked_expression_unit", "ocr_context_unit", "source_page_unit"):
0241 |         for row in _by_ids(db, "sense_attestation", column, roles):
0242 |             attestations[row["id"]] = row
0243 |     for part in _by_ids(db, "term_expression_part", "unit_id", roles):
0244 |         for row in _by_ids(db, "sense_attestation", "id", [part["attestation_id"]]):
0245 |             attestations[row["id"]] = row
0246 |     for attestation in attestations.values():
0247 |         parts = _by_ids(db, "term_expression_part", "attestation_id", [attestation["id"]])
0248 |         expression_ids = [attestation.get("checked_expression_unit")] + [p["unit_id"] for p in parts]
0249 |         direct = []
0250 |         for expression_id in filter(None, expression_ids):
0251 |             row = _unit(db, expression_id)
0252 |             if row and row["version_id"] == selected["version_id"] and row["start"] < selected["end"] and row["end"] > selected["start"]:
0253 |                 direct.append({"unit_id": expression_id, "relation": _spatial(selected, row)})
0254 |         scope = "exact" if any(link["relation"] == "exact_span" for link in direct) else "context"
0255 |         sense = _one(db, "SELECT * FROM term_sense WHERE id=?", (attestation["sense_id"],))
0256 |         lexeme = _one(db, "SELECT * FROM lexeme WHERE id=?", (sense["lexeme_id"],)) if sense else None
0257 |         terms.append({"scope": scope, "match": "exact_attested_expression" if scope == "exact" else "attested_expression_overlap" if direct else "attestation_context", "expression_links": direct, "attestation": attestation, "sense": sense, "lexeme": lexeme})
0258 |         evidence_links.append({"evidence_id": attestation["evidence_id"], "unit_id": attestation.get("checked_expression_unit") or attestation["checked_context_unit"], "scope": scope, "via": "sense_attestation"})
0259 |         if sense and sense.get("decision_id"):
0260 |             decision_links.append({"decision_id": sense["decision_id"], "unit_id": selected["id"], "scope": scope, "via": "sense_attestation"})
0261 |     families = []
0262 |     entry_ids = []
0263 |     occurrences = _by_ids(db, "term_occurrence", "unit_id", roles)
0264 |     for occurrence in occurrences:
0265 |         families.append({**occurrence, **roles[occurrence["unit_id"]], "family": _one(db, "SELECT * FROM term_family WHERE id=?", (occurrence["family_id"],))})
0266 |         entry_ids += [r["entry_id"] for r in _by_ids(db, "term_entry_family", "family_id", [occurrence["family_id"]])]
0267 |     entries = [entity(db, "term_entry", i) for i in sorted(set(entry_ids))]
0268 |     for entry in entries:
0269 |         decision_links.append({"decision_id": entry["record"]["decision_id"], "unit_id": selected["id"], "scope": "context", "via": "term_family"})
0270 |     decisions = []
0271 |     for decision in _by_ids(db, "decision", "id", [r["decision_id"] for r in decision_links]):
0272 |         links = []
0273 |         for link in decision_links:
0274 |             if link["decision_id"] == decision["id"]:
0275 |                 links.append({**roles.get(link["unit_id"], {}), **link})
0276 |         decisions.append({**decision, "links": links})
0277 |         for link in _by_ids(db, "decision_evidence", "decision_id", [decision["id"]]):
0278 |             evidence_links.append({"evidence_id": link["evidence_id"], "scope": "context", "via": "decision", "decision_id": decision["id"]})
0279 |     evidence = []
0280 |     for evidence_id in sorted({r["evidence_id"] for r in evidence_links}):
0281 |         record = _evidence(db, evidence_id)
0282 |         if record:
0283 |             links = [{**roles.get(r.get("unit_id"), {}), **r} for r in evidence_links if r["evidence_id"] == evidence_id]
0284 |             evidence.append({**record, "links": links})
0285 |     # Form lookup is discovery, never a sense assignment. This also exposes the
0286 |     # curated lexeme behind an older family annotation in an aligned passage.
0287 |     discoveries = []
0288 |     forms = {r["query_form"].casefold() for r in occurrences}
0289 |     if selected["kind"] in ("word", "term_expression", "term_occurrence"):
0290 |         forms.add(selected["text"].casefold())
0291 |     if forms and _has(db, "lexeme_form"):
0292 |         discovered = {r["lexeme_id"] for r in _rows(db, "SELECT lexeme_id,query_key,form FROM lexeme_form") if r["query_key"].casefold() in forms or r["form"].casefold() in forms}
0293 |         bound = {r["lexeme"]["id"] for r in terms if r["lexeme"]}
0294 |         discoveries = [{"lexeme_id": i, "scope": "context", "match": "form_discovery_not_sense_assignment"} for i in sorted(discovered - bound)]
0295 |     lexeme_ids = {r["lexeme"]["id"] for r in terms if r["lexeme"]} | {r["lexeme_id"] for r in discoveries}
0296 |     versions = {m["unit"]["version_id"] for a in alignments for m in a["members"] if m["unit"] and m["unit"]["version_id"] != selected["version_id"]}
0297 |     targets = list(roles) + [selected["version_id"]] + list(versions) + [r["id"] for r in decisions] + list(lexeme_ids) + [r["sense"]["id"] for r in terms if r["sense"]]
0298 |     return {"unit": selected, "analyses": _by_ids(db, "analysis", "version_id", [selected["version_id"]] + list(versions)), "ancestors": ancestors, "annotations": [{**row, **roles[row["id"]]} for row in annotations], "context_links": list(roles.values()), "aligned_context": list(counterpart_rows.values()), "decisions": decisions, "evidence": evidence, "terms": terms, "term_families": families, "term_entries": entries, "term_discoveries": discoveries, "term_objects": [entity(db, "lexeme", i) for i in sorted(lexeme_ids)], "alignments": alignments, "alternatives": _by_ids(db, "text_version", "id", versions), "history": _history(db, targets), "note": "Exact labels describe linked spans, not certified semantic equivalence. Containing, overlapping, aligned and form-discovery links remain contextual."}
0299 | 
0300 | 
0301 | def _match_span(body, needle, start=0):
0302 |     folded = body.casefold()
0303 |     index = folded.find(needle.casefold(), start)
0304 |     if index < 0:
0305 |         return None
0306 |     end = index + len(needle.casefold())
0307 |     position, first, last = 0, None, None
0308 |     for i, char in enumerate(body):
0309 |         next_position = position + len(char.casefold())
0310 |         if first is None and next_position > index:
0311 |             first = i
0312 |         if next_position >= end:
0313 |             last = i + 1
0314 |             break
0315 |         position = next_position
0316 |     return first, last, end
0317 | 
0318 | 
0319 | def _snippet(body, start, end, width=110):
0320 |     a, b = max(0, start - width), min(len(body), end + width)
0321 |     return ("…" if a else "") + body[a:b] + ("…" if b < len(body) else "")
0322 | 
0323 | 
0324 | def search(db, text, limit=30):
0325 |     """Literal Unicode-casefolded German/English search, without FTS writes."""
0326 |     if not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip():
0327 |         return []
0328 |     if isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, int) or not 1 <= limit <= 200:
0329 |         raise ValueError("Search limit must be an integer from 1 to 200")
0330 |     needle = text.strip()
0331 |     if len(needle) > 500:
0332 |         raise ValueError("Search text must be 500 characters or fewer")
0333 |     folded = needle.casefold()
0334 |     term_results, sense_results, text_results = [], [], []
0335 |     if _has(db, "lexeme"):
0336 |         all_forms = defaultdict(list)
0337 |         for row in _rows(db, "SELECT * FROM lexeme_form") if _has(db, "lexeme_form") else []:
0338 |             all_forms[row["lexeme_id"]].append(row["form"])
0339 |         for row in _rows(db, "SELECT * FROM lexeme ORDER BY headword,id"):
0340 |             values = [row["headword"]] + all_forms[row["id"]]
0341 |             matches = [v for v in values if folded in v.casefold()]
0342 |             if matches:
0343 |                 term_results.append({"kind": "lexeme", "id": row["id"], "label": row["headword"], "language": "de", "match_field": "headword/form", "matches": list(dict.fromkeys(matches)), "snippet": row["headword"], "exact_form": any(v.casefold() == folded for v in matches)})
0344 |         term_results.sort(key=lambda r: (not r["exact_form"], r["label"], r["id"]))
0345 |     if _has(db, "term_sense"):
0346 |         renderings = defaultdict(list)
0347 |         for row in _rows(db, "SELECT * FROM sense_rendering ORDER BY rank") if _has(db, "sense_rendering") else []:
0348 |             renderings[row["sense_id"]].append(row["rendering"])
0349 |         for row in _rows(db, "SELECT s.*,l.headword FROM term_sense s JOIN lexeme l ON l.id=s.lexeme_id ORDER BY l.headword,s.id"):
0350 |             fields = {"English rendering": " / ".join(renderings[row["id"]]), "definition": row["definition"], "rule": row["rule"], "caution": row["caution"]}
0351 |             for field, value in fields.items():
0352 |                 match = _match_span(value, needle)
0353 |                 if match:
0354 |                     sense_results.append({"kind": "term_sense", "id": row["id"], "lexeme_id": row["lexeme_id"], "label": row["headword"] + " — " + row["label"], "language": "en", "match_field": field, "snippet": _snippet(value, *match[:2]), "renderings": renderings[row["id"]], "exact_form": field == "English rendering" and any(v.casefold() == folded for v in renderings[row["id"]]), "rendering_rank": next((i for i,v in enumerate(renderings[row["id"]],1) if v.casefold()==folded), 999)})
0355 |                     break
0356 |     if _has(db, "text_version"):
0357 |         by_version = []
0358 |         for version in _rows(db, "SELECT * FROM text_version ORDER BY CASE WHEN transcription_status LIKE '%uncorrected%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,language,id"):
0359 |             matches, offset = [], 0
0360 |             for _ in range(min(limit, 5)):
0361 |                 match = _match_span(version["body"], needle, offset)
0362 |                 if not match:
0363 |                     break
0364 |                 a, b, offset = match
0365 |                 unit = _one(db, "SELECT id,kind FROM unit WHERE version_id=? AND start<=? AND end>=? ORDER BY (end-start), CASE kind WHEN 'word' THEN 0 WHEN 'term_expression' THEN 1 WHEN 'phrase' THEN 2 WHEN 'sentence' THEN 3 WHEN 'rendering' THEN 3 WHEN 'paragraph' THEN 4 ELSE 5 END,id LIMIT 1", (version["id"], a, b))
0366 |                 matches.append({"kind": "text_version", "id": version["id"], "label": version["title"], "language": version["language"], "match_field": "embedded text", "snippet": _snippet(version["body"], a, b), "match_start": a, "match_end": b, "unit_id": unit["id"] if unit else None, "locator": version["locator"], "transcription_status": version["transcription_status"]})
0367 |             by_version.append(matches)
0368 |         while any(by_version):
0369 |             for matches in by_version:
0370 |                 if matches:
0371 |                     text_results.append(matches.pop(0))
0372 |     # Exact lexical/rendering matches precede incidental prose. Other channels
0373 |     # retain bounded visibility; an English text hit is never a sense assignment.
0374 |     exact = [r for r in term_results + sense_results if r.get("exact_form")]
0375 |     exact.sort(key=lambda r: (r["kind"] != "lexeme", r.get("rendering_rank", 0), r["label"], r["id"]))
0376 |     curated = [r for r in term_results + sense_results if not r.get("exact_form")]
0377 |     curated.sort(key=lambda r: (r["match_field"] not in ("headword/form", "English rendering"), r["label"], r["id"]))
0378 |     return (exact + curated + text_results)[:limit]
0379 | 
0380 | 
0381 | 
0382 | def _section_numbers(db):
0383 |     versions = _rows(db, "SELECT id,title,language,locator,transcription_status FROM text_version ORDER BY id")
0384 |     by_id = {v["id"]: set(re.findall(r"§\s*(\d+)\b", v["title"])) for v in versions}
0385 |     for row in _rows(db, "SELECT DISTINCT version_id,label FROM unit WHERE kind='paragraph'"):
0386 |         number = re.fullmatch(r"\s*§\s*(\d+)\s*", row["label"])
0387 |         if number and not by_id.get(row['version_id']):
0388 |             by_id.setdefault(row["version_id"], set()).add(number[1])
0389 |     declared = metadata(db).get("passages", [])
0390 |     for passage_info in declared if isinstance(declared, list) else []:
0391 |         if not isinstance(passage_info, dict):
0392 |             continue
0393 |         section = str(passage_info.get("section", "")).strip().removeprefix("§").strip()
0394 |         version_ids = passage_info.get("version_ids", [])
0395 |         if re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,6}", section) and isinstance(version_ids, list):
0396 |             for version_id in version_ids:
0397 |                 if isinstance(version_id, str) and version_id in by_id:
0398 |                     by_id[version_id].add(section)
0399 |     return versions, by_id
0400 | 
0401 | 
0402 | def sections(db):
0403 |     """Discover explicit passage metadata plus legacy title/paragraph labels."""
0404 |     versions, numbers = _section_numbers(db)
0405 |     groups = defaultdict(list)
0406 |     for version in versions:
0407 |         for number in numbers[version["id"]]:
0408 |             groups[number].append(version)
0409 |     return [{"section": number, "label": "§" + number, "versions": groups[number]} for number in sorted(groups, key=int)]
0410 | 
0411 | 
0412 | def _blocks(db, version_id):
0413 |     units = _rows(db, "SELECT u.*, substr(v.body,u.start+1,u.end-u.start) AS text FROM unit u JOIN text_version v ON v.id=u.version_id WHERE u.version_id=? AND u.kind IN ('rendering','sentence','paragraph','excerpt') ORDER BY u.start,u.end,u.id", (version_id,))
0414 |     for kind in ("rendering", "sentence", "paragraph", "excerpt"):
0415 |         chosen = [u for u in units if u["kind"] == kind]
0416 |         if chosen:
0417 |             break
0418 |     else:
0419 |         version = _one(db, "SELECT body FROM text_version WHERE id=?", (version_id,))
0420 |         return [{"id": None, "version_id": version_id, "kind": "unsegmented text", "label": "Embedded text; no recorded segmentation", "start": 0, "end": len(version["body"]), "text": version["body"], "words": []}]
0421 |     words = _rows(db, "SELECT u.*,substr(v.body,u.start+1,u.end-u.start) AS text FROM unit u JOIN text_version v ON v.id=u.version_id WHERE u.version_id=? AND u.kind IN ('word','written_word_candidate') ORDER BY u.start,u.end,u.id", (version_id,))
0422 |     result, spans = [], set()
0423 |     for block in chosen:
0424 |         span = block["start"], block["end"]
0425 |         if span in spans:
0426 |             continue
0427 |         spans.add(span)
0428 |         block["words"] = [w for w in words if block["start"] <= w["start"] and w["end"] <= block["end"]]
0429 |         result.append(block)
0430 |     return result
0431 | 
0432 | 
0433 | def passage(db, section):
0434 |     value = str(section).strip().removeprefix("§").strip()
0435 |     if not re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,6}", value):
0436 |         raise ValueError("Section must be a number, for example 147 or §147")
0437 |     versions, numbers = _section_numbers(db)
0438 |     selected = [v for v in versions if value in numbers[v["id"]]]
0439 |     if not selected:
0440 |         raise NotFound("No recorded passage for §" + value)
0441 |     output = []
0442 |     for version_metadata in selected:
0443 |         row = _one(db, "SELECT * FROM text_version WHERE id=?", (version_metadata["id"],))
0444 |         output.append({**row, "analyses": _by_ids(db, "analysis", "version_id", [row["id"]]), "blocks": _blocks(db, row["id"]), "history": _history(db, [row["id"]], "text_version")})
0445 |     ids = {v["id"] for v in output}
0446 |     alignments = []
0447 |     if _has(db, "alignment"):
0448 |         for row in _rows(db, "SELECT * FROM alignment ORDER BY id"):
0449 |             if row["left_version"] in ids and row["right_version"] in ids:
0450 |                 alignments.append({**row, "members": _alignment_members(db, row["id"])})
0451 |     defaults = next((r for r in metadata(db).get('reading_defaults', []) if r.get('section') == value), {})
0452 |     return {"section": value, "label": "§" + value, "versions": output, "alignments": alignments, "reading_defaults": defaults, "note": "Version selection is a reading choice, not acceptance or certification. Alignments retain their recorded relation and status."}
0453 | 
0454 | 
0455 | def metadata(db):
0456 |     return {r["key"]: _json(r["value_json"], r["value_json"]) for r in _rows(db, "SELECT * FROM build_metadata")} if _has(db, "build_metadata") else {}
0457 | 
0458 | 
0459 | def resources(db):
0460 |     """Return source references; filesystem authorization belongs to the reader."""
0461 |     records = []
0462 |     for table, sha in (("text_version", "source_sha256"), ("evidence", "source_sha256")):
0463 |         if _has(db, table):
0464 |             records.extend({"path": r["source_path"], "sha256": r[sha], "origin": table} for r in _rows(db, f'SELECT DISTINCT source_path,{sha} FROM {table}'))
0465 |     declared = metadata(db).get("resources", [])
0466 |     if isinstance(declared, list):
0467 |         records.extend({**r, "origin": "manifest"} for r in declared if isinstance(r, dict) and isinstance(r.get("path"), str))
0468 |     return records
```

```text
0001 | """Small record, path and Unicode helpers; no network or corpus dependency."""
0002 | from pathlib import Path
0003 | import hashlib
0004 | import json
0005 | import os
0006 | import re
0007 | import unicodedata
0008 | import uuid
0009 | 
0010 | 
0011 | def canonical(value):
0012 |     return json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False)
0013 | 
0014 | 
0015 | def fingerprint(value):
0016 |     return hashlib.sha256(canonical(value).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
0017 | 
0018 | 
0019 | def file_hash(path):
0020 |     return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
0021 | 
0022 | 
0023 | def parse_json(value):
0024 |     def unique(items):
0025 |         result={}
0026 |         for key,value in items:
0027 |             if key in result:raise ValueError(f'duplicate JSON key: {key}')
0028 |             result[key]=value
0029 |         return result
0030 |     def invalid(value):raise ValueError('non-finite JSON number: '+value)
0031 |     return json.loads(value,object_pairs_hook=unique,parse_constant=invalid)
0032 | 
0033 | 
0034 | def read_json(path):
0035 |     return parse_json(Path(path).read_bytes())
0036 | 
0037 | 
0038 | def within(root,relative):
0039 |     root=Path(root).resolve();path=Path(relative)
0040 |     if path.is_absolute() or '..' in path.parts:raise ValueError('path must be relative and confined: '+str(relative))
0041 |     target=(root/path).resolve()
0042 |     if not target.is_relative_to(root):raise ValueError('path leaves project: '+str(relative))
0043 |     return target

0046 | def sync_directory(path):
0047 |     """Persist a published directory entry where the OS supports directory fsync."""
0048 |     try:fd=os.open(str(path),os.O_RDONLY|getattr(os,'O_DIRECTORY',0))
0049 |     except OSError:
0050 |         if os.name=='nt':return
0051 |         raise
0052 |     try:os.fsync(fd)
0053 |     finally:os.close(fd)

0056 | def atomic_json(path,value,*,replace=True):
0057 |     path=Path(path);path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
0058 |     temporary=path.with_name('.'+path.name+'.'+uuid.uuid4().hex+'.tmp')
0059 |     try:
0060 |         with temporary.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as stream:
0061 |             stream.write(json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n')
0062 |             stream.flush();os.fsync(stream.fileno())
0063 |         if replace:os.replace(temporary,path)
0064 |         else:os.link(temporary,path);temporary.unlink()
0065 |         sync_directory(path.parent)
0066 |     finally:
0067 |         if temporary.exists():temporary.unlink()

0069 | 
0070 | def word_spans(body):
0071 |     """Unicode L/M/N written tokens; internal hyphens/apostrophes retained.
0072 | 
0073 |     This freezes the old token convention, not a linguistic analysis. Offsets
0074 |     count Python Unicode code points; source text is never normalized here.
0075 |     """
0076 |     def constituent(char):return unicodedata.category(char)[0] in 'LMN'
0077 |     i=0
0078 |     while i<len(body):
0079 |         if not constituent(body[i]):i+=1;continue
0080 |         start=i;i+=1
0081 |         while i<len(body):
0082 |             if constituent(body[i]):i+=1
0083 |             elif body[i] in "-'’" and i+1<len(body) and constituent(body[i+1]):i+=2
0084 |             else:break
0085 |         yield start,i
0086 | 
0087 | 
0088 | def validate_shape(value,schema,where='$',root=None):
0089 |     """Validate the documented finite schema vocabulary used by this package.
0090 | 
0091 |     Supports local refs, scalar/object/array types and the constraints below;
0092 |     rejects unknown schema keywords instead of claiming arbitrary JSON Schema
0093 |     support. SQL validates relational constraints in addition to these shapes.
0094 |     """
0095 |     root=schema if root is None else root
0096 |     allowed={'$schema','$id','$defs','title','description','$ref','type','const','enum','required','properties','additionalProperties','items','minItems','minLength','minimum','pattern'}
0097 |     unknown=set(schema)-allowed
0098 |     if unknown:raise ValueError(f'unsupported schema keywords {where}: {sorted(unknown)}')
0099 |     if '$ref' in schema:
0100 |         ref=schema['$ref']
0101 |         if not ref.startswith('#/'):raise ValueError('only local schema references are supported')
0102 |         target=root
0103 |         for part in ref[2:].split('/'):target=target[part.replace('~1','/').replace('~0','~')]
0104 |         return validate_shape(value,target,where,root)
0105 |     types=schema.get('type');types=types if isinstance(types,list) else [types] if types else []
0106 |     checks={'object':lambda x:isinstance(x,dict),'array':lambda x:isinstance(x,list),'string':lambda x:isinstance(x,str),'integer':lambda x:isinstance(x,int) and not isinstance(x,bool),'number':lambda x:isinstance(x,(int,float)) and not isinstance(x,bool),'boolean':lambda x:isinstance(x,bool),'null':lambda x:x is None}
0107 |     if types and not any(checks[t](value) for t in types):raise ValueError(f'{where}: expected {types}')
0108 |     if 'const' in schema and canonical(value)!=canonical(schema['const']):raise ValueError(f'{where}: expected {schema["const"]!r}')
0109 |     if 'enum' in schema and not any(canonical(value)==canonical(option) for option in schema['enum']):raise ValueError(f'{where}: value is not in enum')
0110 |     if isinstance(value,str):
0111 |         if len(value)<schema.get('minLength',0):raise ValueError(f'{where}: text is empty or too short')
0112 |         if 'pattern' in schema and re.search(schema['pattern'],value) is None:raise ValueError(f'{where}: pattern mismatch')
0113 |     if isinstance(value,(int,float)) and not isinstance(value,bool) and 'minimum' in schema and value<schema['minimum']:raise ValueError(f'{where}: below minimum')
0114 |     if isinstance(value,list):
0115 |         if len(value)<schema.get('minItems',0):raise ValueError(f'{where}: array too short')
0116 |         for n,item in enumerate(value):
0117 |             if 'items' in schema:validate_shape(item,schema['items'],f'{where}[{n}]',root)
0118 |     if isinstance(value,dict):
0119 |         for key in schema.get('required',[]):
0120 |             if key not in value:raise ValueError(f'{where}: missing {key}')
0121 |         properties=schema.get('properties',{})
0122 |         for key,item in value.items():
0123 |             if key in properties:validate_shape(item,properties[key],where+'.'+key,root)
0124 |             elif schema.get('additionalProperties') is False:raise ValueError(f'{where}: unknown property {key}')
0125 |             elif isinstance(schema.get('additionalProperties'),dict):validate_shape(item,schema['additionalProperties'],where+'.'+key,root)
```


### FILE: src/humboldt/release.py
```text
0001 | """Portable local releases and deterministic logical exports; no publishing."""
0002 | import csv
0003 | from datetime import datetime,timezone
0004 | import json
0005 | from pathlib import Path
0006 | import shutil
0007 | import sqlite3
0008 | import subprocess
0009 | import sys
0010 | import tempfile
0011 | 
0012 | from . import __version__,database,ingest
0013 | from .records import atomic_json,canonical,file_hash,read_json
0014 | 
0015 | 
0016 | def export(db,output,*,table=None,format='json'):
0017 |     output=Path(output)
0018 |     if output.exists():raise ValueError('export output already exists')
0019 |     names={r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
0020 |     if table and table not in names:raise ValueError('unknown export table')
0021 |     if format=='csv' and not table:raise ValueError('CSV export requires --table')
0022 |     selected=[table] if table else sorted(names-{'schema_migration','build_metadata'})
0023 |     data={}
0024 |     for name in selected:
0025 |         # Membership in sqlite_master, never unchecked SQL interpolation.
0026 |         quoted='"'+name.replace('"','""')+'"'
0027 |         data[name]=sorted([dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM '+quoted)],key=canonical)
0028 |     output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
0029 |     with output.open('x',encoding='utf-8',newline='') as stream:
0030 |         if format=='json':json.dump(dict(schema_version=db.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0],logical_digest=database.logical_digest(db),tables=data),stream,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,indent=2);stream.write('\n')
0031 |         else:
0032 |             fields=[r[1] for r in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info("'+table.replace('"','""')+'")')]
0033 |             writer=csv.DictWriter(stream,fieldnames=fields);writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(data[table])
0034 | 
0035 | 
0036 | def create(project,db_path,output):
0037 |     project=ingest.project_root(project);db_path=Path(db_path).resolve();output=Path(output).resolve()
0038 |     if output.exists():raise ValueError('release destination already exists')
0039 |     if output.is_relative_to(project) and output.parts[len(project.parts)]!='releases':raise ValueError('local releases must be under releases/ or a fresh external directory')
0040 |     if not db_path.is_file():raise ValueError('build the database before creating a release')
0041 |     from .revisions import project_lock
0042 |     with project_lock(project):
0043 |         validation=ingest.validate(project)
0044 |         db=database.connect(db_path,readonly=True)
0045 |         try:
0046 |             if database.logical_digest(db)!=validation['logical_digest']:raise ValueError('database is stale; rebuild before release')
0047 |             identity=db.execute("SELECT value_json FROM build_metadata WHERE key='input_digest'").fetchone()
0048 |             if not identity or json.loads(identity[0])!=validation['input_digest']:raise ValueError('build inputs or journal changed; rebuild before release')
0049 |             if not database.report(db)['sealed']:raise ValueError('release database must be sealed')
0050 |             digest=database.logical_digest(db)
0051 |         finally:db.close()
0052 |         output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
0053 |         stage=Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.'+output.name+'.',dir=output.parent))
0054 |         try:
0055 |             shutil.copytree(project/'src',stage/'src',ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns('__pycache__','*.pyc'))
0056 |             shutil.copytree(project/'edition',stage/'edition',ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns('*.tmp'))
0057 |             shutil.copy2(project/'humboldt',stage/'humboldt');shutil.copy2(project/'pyproject.toml',stage/'pyproject.toml')
0058 |             (stage/'build').mkdir();shutil.copy2(db_path,stage/'build/edition.sqlite')
0059 |             (stage/'docs').mkdir()
0060 |             guide=project/'docs/architecture/user-guide.md'
0061 |             if guide.exists():shutil.copy2(guide,stage/'docs/user-guide.md')
0062 |             else:(stage/'docs/user-guide.md').write_text('# Reader and editing guide\n\nRun `python3 humboldt --help` for the local commands. Proposals require explicit attribution and evidence; accepting a proposal records a new event. Rebuild after acceptance. Original records remain preserved in edition data. Text corrections create a new version and require review of affected links.\n',encoding='utf-8')
0063 |             template=project/'tests/fixtures/minimal/edition/passages/minimal.json'
0064 |             if not template.exists():template=project/'examples/minimal-passage.json'
0065 |             if template.exists():
0066 |                 (stage/'examples').mkdir()
0067 |                 shutil.copy2(template,stage/'examples/minimal-passage.json')
0068 |             (stage/'README.md').write_text('# Humboldt research edition\n\nThis is a local frozen research snapshot, not a certified critical edition. Original-source attachments may be absent; checked texts, evidence, alternatives and provenance remain embedded.\n\nWith Python3.11 or newer, run:\n\n```bash\npython3 humboldt validate\npython3 humboldt search understanding\npython3 humboldt passage 147\npython3 humboldt browse\n```\n\nThe browser prints a loopback URL. No model credentials or network are needed. Source and meaning validation retain the statuses recorded in the data. See docs/user-guide.md for editing a working copy. release.json records file hashes and the logical database identity.\n',encoding='utf-8')
0069 |             # Prove the copy stands alone before making it visible.
0070 |             process=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'humboldt','validate'],cwd=stage,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=60)
0071 |             if process.returncode:raise ValueError('portable reader validation failed: '+process.stderr.strip())
0072 |             copied=json.loads(process.stdout)
0073 |             if copied['logical_digest']!=digest:raise ValueError('portable copy differs from original logical data')
0074 |             files={str(p.relative_to(stage)):file_hash(p) for p in sorted(stage.rglob('*')) if p.is_file() and '__pycache__' not in p.parts}
0075 |             atomic_json(stage/'release.json',dict(format='humboldt.release',schema_version=1,software_version=__version__,recorded_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),logical_digest=digest,files=files,limits=['Provisional research translation and model-assisted source checks; no specialist certification.','Raw source attachments are optional and not bundled by default.']))
0076 |             if output.exists():raise ValueError('release destination appeared during packaging')
0077 |             stage.rename(output)
0078 |         except Exception:
0079 |             shutil.rmtree(stage);raise
0080 |     return dict(output=str(output),logical_digest=digest,files=len(files))
```

```text
0027 | body{font:18px/1.55 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:1250px;margin:2rem auto;padding:0 1.3rem;color:#202830;background:#fbfaf6}a{color:#165d87}nav{display:flex;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap;border-bottom:1px solid #bbb;padding-bottom:.8rem}h1{font-size:1.65rem}h2{font-size:1.3rem}h3{font-size:1.1rem}form{margin:1rem 0}input,select,textarea,button{font:inherit;max-width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:.35rem}textarea{display:block;width:100%;min-height:9rem}label{display:block;margin:.6rem 0}pre{white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:.82rem;background:#f0f1ef;padding:.8rem}summary{cursor:pointer}details{margin:.6rem 0}blockquote{margin:.6rem 0;border-left:3px solid #aab;padding:.6rem 1rem;white-space:pre-wrap}.muted{color:#52626b;font-size:.9rem}.tag{font-size:.8rem;background:#e8edf0;border-radius:3px;padding:.15rem .35rem}.notice{background:#fff0d8;padding:.8rem}.pair{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.5rem;border-top:1px solid #ccc;padding:.8rem 0}.words{white-space:pre-wrap}.word{text-decoration:none;border-bottom:1px dotted #8496a0}.word:hover{background:#deedf5}.card{padding:.6rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid #ccc}small{font-size:.8rem}@media(max-width:760px){.pair{grid-template-columns:1fr}}

0031 | def sources(items):
0032 |     if not items:
0033 |         return ''
0034 |     out = '<details><summary>Source attachments</summary><ul>'
0035 |     for item in items:
0036 |         path = item.get('path', '')
0037 |         label = item.get('label') or path or 'Unspecified source'

0039 |         if not item.get('available'):

0041 |         out += '</li>'
0042 |     return out + '</ul></details>'

0044 | 
0045 | def words(block):
0046 |     text = block['text']
0047 |     out, offset = [], 0
0048 |     for word in sorted(block.get('words', []), key=lambda w: (w['start'], w['end'], w['id'])):
0049 |         a, b = word['start'] - block['start'], word['end'] - block['start']
0050 |         if a < offset or b <= a or b > len(text):
0051 |             continue
0052 |         out += [esc(text[offset:a]), '<a class="word" href="' + esc(url('/trace', id=word['id'])) + '">' + esc(text[a:b]) + '</a>']
0053 |         offset = b
0054 |     out.append(esc(text[offset:]))
0055 |     return '<div class="words">' + ''.join(out) + '</div>'
```
