### FILE: src/humboldt/revisions.py
```text
0001 | """Attributed, append-only revisions and a pure projection of their journal.
0002 | 
0003 | An accepted text edit creates a new version and a proposed mechanical analysis.
0004 | Old units and semantic links stay on their old version. Reattachment candidates
0005 | are observations about strings, not claims of semantic equivalence.
0006 | """
0007 | 
0008 | from __future__ import annotations
0009 | 
0010 | from contextlib import contextmanager
0011 | from copy import deepcopy
0012 | from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
0013 | import hashlib
0014 | import json
0015 | import os
0016 | from pathlib import Path
0017 | import re
0018 | import tempfile
0019 | import uuid
0020 | 
0021 | from .records import parse_json, sync_directory, word_spans
0022 | 
0023 | 
0024 | FORMAT = "humboldt.revision_event"
0025 | SCHEMA_VERSION = 1
0026 | SUPPORTED = {
0027 |     "text_version": frozenset({"body"}),
0028 |     "term_sense": frozenset({"rule", "definition", "caution"}),
0029 |     "decision": frozenset({"resolution"}),
0030 | }
0031 | _HEX = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}\Z")
0032 | _MAX_CANDIDATES = 32
0033 | 
0034 | 
0035 | class RevisionError(ValueError):
0036 |     """An unsupported or malformed revision/journal."""
0037 | 
0038 | 
0039 | class RevisionConflict(RevisionError):
0040 |     """The original target precondition is no longer true."""
0041 | 
0042 | 
0043 | class ProjectLocked(RevisionError):
0044 |     """Another writer owns the project lock; no stale lock is removed."""
0045 | 
0046 | 
0047 | def _json(value):
0048 |     try:
0049 |         return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
0050 |                           separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
0051 |     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
0052 |         raise RevisionError("revision data must be finite JSON values") from exc
0053 | 
0054 | 
0055 | def _bytes(value):
0056 |     try:
0057 |         return (_json(value) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
0058 |     except UnicodeError as exc:
0059 |         raise RevisionError("revision data must be valid UTF-8 text") from exc
0060 | 
0061 | 
0062 | def fingerprint(row):
0063 |     """SHA-256 of a complete target row, with canonical JSON keys and values.
0064 | 
0065 |     A UI may include this as expected_sha256 in a downloaded proposal. Do not
0066 |     hash a query wrapper, selected columns, or history instead of the actual row.
0067 |     """
0068 |     if not isinstance(row, dict):
0069 |         row = dict(row)
0070 |     return hashlib.sha256(_bytes(row)).hexdigest()
0071 | 
0072 | 
0073 | def _text(value, name, *, empty=False):
0074 |     if not isinstance(value, str) or "\x00" in value:
0075 |         raise RevisionError(f"{name} must be a string without NUL")
0076 |     if not empty and not value.strip():
0077 |         raise RevisionError(f"{name} must not be empty")
0078 |     try:
0079 |         value.encode("utf-8")
0080 |     except UnicodeError as exc:
0081 |         raise RevisionError(f"{name} must be valid UTF-8") from exc
0082 |     return value
0083 | 
0084 | 
0085 | def _uuid(value, name="event id"):
0086 |     _text(value, name)
0087 |     try:
0088 |         valid = str(uuid.UUID(value)) == value
0089 |     except ValueError:
0090 |         valid = False
0091 |     if not valid:
0092 |         raise RevisionError(f"{name} must be a canonical UUID")
0093 | 
0094 | 
0095 | def _time(value):
0096 |     _text(value, "recorded_at")
0097 |     try:
0098 |         result = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
0099 |     except ValueError as exc:
0100 |         raise RevisionError("recorded_at must be an ISO timestamp") from exc
0101 |     if result.tzinfo is None or result.utcoffset() is None:
0102 |         raise RevisionError("recorded_at must include a timezone")
0103 |     return result.astimezone(timezone.utc)
0104 | 
0105 | 
0106 | def _project(project):
0107 |     path = Path(project).resolve()
0108 |     if not path.is_dir():
0109 |         raise RevisionError("project must be an existing directory")
0110 |     return path
0111 | 
0112 | 
0113 | def _confined(project, relative):
0114 |     result = (project / relative).resolve()
0115 |     if not result.is_relative_to(project):
0116 |         raise RevisionError("journal path escapes the project")
0117 |     return result
0118 | 
0119 | 
0120 | @contextmanager
0121 | def project_lock(project):
0122 |     """Fail-fast exclusive lock shared by all project writers.
0123 | 
0124 |     The owner removes only its own unchanged lock file. There is deliberately
0125 |     no timeout, PID-based recovery, or automatic deletion of an existing lock.
0126 |     """
0127 |     project = _project(project)
0128 |     path = project / ".humboldt.lock"
0129 |     token = {"id": str(uuid.uuid4()), "pid": os.getpid(),
0130 |              "recorded_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
0131 |     try:
0132 |         fd = os.open(path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
0133 |     except FileExistsError as exc:
0134 |         raise ProjectLocked(f"project is locked: {path}") from exc
0135 |     inode = None
0136 |     try:
0137 |         with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
0138 |             stream.write(_bytes(token))
0139 |             stream.flush()
0140 |             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
0141 |             inode = os.fstat(stream.fileno()).st_ino
0142 |         yield
0143 |     finally:
0144 |         try:
0145 |             current = path.stat()
0146 |             same = current.st_ino == inode and path.read_bytes() == _bytes(token)
0147 |         except FileNotFoundError:
0148 |             same = False
0149 |         if same:
0150 |             path.unlink()
0151 | 
0152 | 
0153 | def _publish(project, directory, event):
0154 |     parent = _confined(project, directory)
0155 |     parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
0156 |     target = parent / (event["id"] + ".json")
0157 |     fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".revision-", suffix=".tmp", dir=parent)
0158 |     try:
0159 |         with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
0160 |             stream.write(_bytes(event))
0161 |             stream.flush()
0162 |             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
0163 |         # A no-replace atomic publication: neither a UUID collision nor another
0164 |         # writer can cause an existing journal record to be overwritten.
0165 |         try:
0166 |             os.link(temporary, target)
0167 |         except FileExistsError as exc:
0168 |             raise RevisionConflict("journal event already exists") from exc
0169 |         sync_directory(parent)
0170 |     finally:
0171 |         Path(temporary).unlink(missing_ok=True)
0172 | 
0173 | 
0174 | def _read_events(project):
0175 |     project = _project(project)
0176 |     events = []
0177 |     for relative, event_type in (("edition/proposals", "proposal"),
0178 |                                  ("edition/changes", "decision")):
0179 |         directory = _confined(project, relative)
0180 |         if not directory.exists():
0181 |             continue
0182 |         for path in sorted(directory.glob("*.json")):
0183 |             if path.is_symlink() or not path.resolve().is_relative_to(project):
0184 |                 raise RevisionError("journal files must be local regular files")
0185 |             try:
0186 |                 raw = path.read_bytes()
0187 |                 event = parse_json(raw)
0188 |             except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
0189 |                 raise RevisionError(f"cannot read journal event: {path.name}") from exc
0190 |             _validate_event(event)
0191 |             if event["event_type"] != event_type or path.stem != event["id"]:
0192 |                 raise RevisionError("journal filename/directory does not match event")
0193 |             if raw != _bytes(event):
0194 |                 raise RevisionError("journal event is not canonical JSON")
0195 |             events.append(event)
0196 |     return _ordered(events)
0197 | 
0198 | 
0199 | def _validate_event(event):
0200 |     if not isinstance(event, dict):
0201 |         raise RevisionError("journal events must be JSON objects")
0202 |     base = {"format", "schema_version", "event_type", "id", "sequence",
0203 |             "recorded_at", "by", "reason", "evidence"}
0204 |     if event.get("event_type") == "proposal":
0205 |         extra = {"kind", "target_id", "field", "value", "old_value", "target_sha256"}
0206 |     elif event.get("event_type") == "decision":
0207 |         extra = {"proposal_id", "accept"}
0208 |     else:
0209 |         raise RevisionError("unsupported event_type")
0210 |     if set(event) != base | extra:
0211 |         raise RevisionError("missing or unsupported journal fields")
0212 |     if event["format"] != FORMAT or type(event["schema_version"]) is not int or event["schema_version"] != SCHEMA_VERSION:
0213 |         raise RevisionError("unsupported revision event format/schema")
0214 |     _uuid(event["id"])
0215 |     if type(event["sequence"]) is not int or event["sequence"] < 1:
0216 |         raise RevisionError("sequence must be a positive integer")
0217 |     _time(event["recorded_at"])
0218 |     _text(event["by"], "by")
0219 |     _text(event["reason"], "reason")
0220 |     if not isinstance(event["evidence"], list):
0221 |         raise RevisionError("evidence must be a list of known evidence IDs")
0222 |     if any(not isinstance(e, str) or not e.strip() for e in event["evidence"]):
0223 |         raise RevisionError("evidence references must be nonempty strings")
0224 |     if len(event["evidence"]) != len(set(event["evidence"])):
0225 |         raise RevisionError("duplicate evidence reference")
0226 |     if event["event_type"] == "decision":
0227 |         _uuid(event["proposal_id"], "proposal_id")
0228 |         if type(event["accept"]) is not bool:
0229 |             raise RevisionError("accept must explicitly be true or false")
0230 |     else:
0231 |         _operation(event["kind"], event["field"])
0232 |         _text(event["target_id"], "target_id")
0233 |         _value(event["kind"], event["field"], event["value"])
0234 |         _text(event["old_value"], "old_value", empty=True)
0235 |         if not isinstance(event["target_sha256"], str) or not _HEX.fullmatch(event["target_sha256"]):
0236 |             raise RevisionError("target_sha256 must be a lowercase SHA-256")
0237 |     _bytes(event)
0238 | 
0239 | 
0240 | def _ordered(events):
0241 |     try:
0242 |         result = list(events)
0243 |     except TypeError as exc:
0244 |         raise RevisionError("events must be an iterable of event objects") from exc
0245 |     for event in result:
0246 |         _validate_event(event)
0247 |     if len({e["id"] for e in result}) != len(result):
0248 |         raise RevisionError("duplicate event UUID")
0249 |     result.sort(key=lambda e: e["sequence"])
0250 |     if [e["sequence"] for e in result] != list(range(1, len(result) + 1)):
0251 |         raise RevisionError("journal sequence is duplicated or incomplete")
0252 |     if any(_time(a["recorded_at"]) > _time(b["recorded_at"])
0253 |            for a, b in zip(result, result[1:])):
0254 |         raise RevisionError("journal time goes backwards")
0255 |     return result
0256 | 
0257 | 
0258 | def _operation(kind, field):
0259 |     _text(kind, "kind")
0260 |     _text(field, "field")
0261 |     if kind not in SUPPORTED or field not in SUPPORTED[kind]:
0262 |         raise RevisionError(f"unsupported revision operation: {kind!r}/{field!r}")
0263 | 
0264 | 
0265 | def _value(kind, field, value):
0266 |     _operation(kind, field)
0267 |     _text(value, "value", empty=(kind == "term_sense" and field == "caution"))
0268 | 
0269 | 
0270 | def _target(tables, kind, target_id):
0271 |     rows = [r for r in tables.get(kind, []) if r.get("id") == target_id]
0272 |     if len(rows) != 1:
0273 |         raise RevisionError(f"target must identify exactly one {kind} row: {target_id}")
0274 |     return rows[0]
0275 | 
0276 | 
0277 | def _evidence(tables, references):
0278 |     known = {r.get("id") for r in tables.get("evidence", [])}
0279 |     if any(ref not in known for ref in references):
0280 |         raise RevisionError("unknown evidence reference; only existing evidence IDs are supported")
0281 | 
0282 | 
0283 | def _text_version(row):
0284 |     for name in ("id", "title", "language", "body", "body_sha256", "source_path",
0285 |                  "source_sha256", "locator", "transcription_status", "provenance"):
0286 |         _text(row.get(name), "text_version." + name,
0287 |               empty=name in {"locator", "transcription_status", "provenance"})
0288 |     if not _HEX.fullmatch(row["source_sha256"]):
0289 |         raise RevisionError("invalid original source_sha256")
0290 |     if row["body_sha256"] != hashlib.sha256(row["body"].encode("utf-8")).hexdigest():
0291 |         raise RevisionError("original text body hash does not match body")
0292 | 
0293 | 
0294 | def _check(tables, proposal, superseded):
0295 |     row = _target(tables, proposal["kind"], proposal["target_id"])
0296 |     if proposal["field"] not in row:
0297 |         raise RevisionError("target field is absent")
0298 |     if proposal["kind"] == "text_version":
0299 |         _text_version(row)
0300 |         if row["id"] in superseded:
0301 |             raise RevisionConflict("text version has an accepted successor; target that version explicitly")
0302 |     if fingerprint(row) != proposal["target_sha256"] or row[proposal["field"]] != proposal["old_value"]:
0303 |         raise RevisionConflict("target no longer matches the proposal's original precondition")
0304 |     return row
0305 | 
0306 | 
0307 | def _next(events, by, reason, evidence):
0308 |     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
0309 |     if events:
0310 |         now = max(now, _time(events[-1]["recorded_at"]) + timedelta(microseconds=1))
0311 |     event_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
0312 |     if any(e["id"] == event_id for e in events):
0313 |         raise RevisionConflict("generated event UUID already exists")
0314 |     return {"format": FORMAT, "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
0315 |             "id": event_id, "sequence": len(events) + 1,
0316 |             "recorded_at": now.isoformat(timespec="microseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z"),
0317 |             "by": _text(by, "by"), "reason": _text(reason, "reason"),
0318 |             "evidence": list(evidence)}
0319 | 
0320 | 
0321 | def _superseded(events):
0322 |     proposals = {e["id"]: e for e in events if e["event_type"] == "proposal"}
0323 |     return {proposals[e["proposal_id"]]["target_id"] for e in events
0324 |             if e["event_type"] == "decision" and e["accept"]
0325 |             and e["proposal_id"] in proposals
0326 |             and proposals[e["proposal_id"]]["kind"] == "text_version"}
0327 | 
0328 | 
0329 | def propose(project, *, kind, target_id, field, value, by, reason, evidence=(),
0330 |             expected_sha256=None):
0331 |     """Persist a proposal for the current target, checking a supplied UI hash.
0332 | 
0333 |     An omitted expected_sha256 creates a proposal against the current snapshot;
0334 |     a supplied stale hash is never silently replaced by a new one.
0335 |     """
0336 |     _operation(kind, field)
0337 |     _text(target_id, "target_id")
0338 |     _value(kind, field, value)
0339 |     if not isinstance(evidence, (list, tuple)):
0340 |         raise RevisionError("evidence must be a list of existing evidence IDs")
0341 |     project = _project(project)
0342 |     with project_lock(project):
0343 |         from . import ingest  # local import: ingest invokes the pure apply()
0344 |         tables, _metadata = ingest.load(project, changes=True)
0345 |         events = _read_events(project)
0346 |         row = _target(tables, kind, target_id)
0347 |         if field not in row:
0348 |             raise RevisionError("target field is absent")
0349 |         if kind == "text_version":
0350 |             _text_version(row)
0351 |             if target_id in _superseded(events):
0352 |                 raise RevisionConflict("text version already has an accepted successor")
0353 |         digest = fingerprint(row)
0354 |         if expected_sha256 is not None and expected_sha256 != digest:
0355 |             raise RevisionConflict("supplied target hash is stale or invalid")
0356 |         if row[field] == value:
0357 |             raise RevisionError("proposal would make no change")
0358 |         event = _next(events, by, reason, evidence)
0359 |         event.update(event_type="proposal", kind=kind, target_id=target_id,
0360 |                      field=field, value=value, old_value=row[field], target_sha256=digest)
0361 |         _validate_event(event)
0362 |         _evidence(tables, event["evidence"])
0363 |         _publish(project, "edition/proposals", event)
0364 |     return event
0365 | 
0366 | 
0367 | def decide(project, proposal_id, *, accept, by, reason):
0368 |     """Record explicit acceptance or rejection; stale acceptance is refused."""
0369 |     _uuid(proposal_id, "proposal_id")
0370 |     if type(accept) is not bool:
0371 |         raise RevisionError("accept must explicitly be true or false")
0372 |     project = _project(project)
0373 |     with project_lock(project):
0374 |         from . import ingest
0375 |         tables, _metadata = ingest.load(project, changes=True)
0376 |         events = _read_events(project)
0377 |         proposals = {e["id"]: e for e in events if e["event_type"] == "proposal"}
0378 |         if proposal_id not in proposals:
0379 |             raise RevisionError("unknown proposal UUID")
0380 |         if any(e["event_type"] == "decision" and e["proposal_id"] == proposal_id for e in events):
0381 |             raise RevisionConflict("proposal already has a decision")
0382 |         proposal = proposals[proposal_id]
0383 |         event = _next(events, by, reason, proposal["evidence"])
0384 |         event.update(event_type="decision", proposal_id=proposal_id, accept=accept)
0385 |         _validate_event(event)
0386 |         if accept:
0387 |             row = _check(tables, proposal, _superseded(events))
0388 |             # Reuse the one-operation pure transformation for preflight. Do not
0389 |             # recursively call apply(), ingest.load(), propose(), or decide().
0390 |             _project_one(deepcopy(tables), proposal, event, deepcopy(row))
0391 |         _publish(project, "edition/changes", event)
0392 |     return event
0393 | 
0394 | 
0395 | def _derived(proposal_id, kind, detail=""):
0396 |     return "revision-" + kind + "-" + str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.UUID(proposal_id), kind + ":" + detail))
0397 | 
0398 | 
0399 | def _append(tables, table, row):
0400 |     if any(r.get("id") == row["id"] for r in tables.get(table, [])):
0401 |         raise RevisionConflict(f"new {table} identity already exists")
0402 |     tables.setdefault(table, []).append(row)
0403 | 
0404 | 
0405 | def _paragraphs(body):
0406 |     start = 0
0407 |     for end, next_start in [(m.start(), m.end()) for m in re.finditer(r"\n[ \t]*\n", body)] + [(len(body), len(body))]:
0408 |         a, b = start, end
0409 |         while a < b and body[a].isspace():
0410 |             a += 1
0411 |         while b > a and body[b - 1].isspace():
0412 |             b -= 1
0413 |         if a < b:
0414 |             yield a, b
0415 |         start = next_start
0416 | 
0417 | 
0418 | def _positions(body, excerpt):
0419 |     found, at = [], 0
0420 |     while True:
0421 |         at = body.find(excerpt, at)
0422 |         if at < 0:
0423 |             return found, False
0424 |         if len(found) == _MAX_CANDIDATES:
0425 |             return found, True
0426 |         found.append({"start": at, "end": at + len(excerpt)})
0427 |         at += 1
0428 | 
0429 | 
0430 | def _impact(decision, kind, entity_id, impact, metadata):
0431 |     return {"revision_id": decision["id"], "entity_kind": kind,
0432 |             "entity_id": entity_id, "impact": impact, "metadata_json": _json(metadata)}
0433 | 
0434 | 
0435 | def _key(row):
0436 |     return row["id"] if isinstance(row.get("id"), str) else _json(row)
0437 | 
0438 | 
0439 | def _references(tables, identifiers, decision, impact):
0440 |     result = []
0441 |     for table, rows in sorted(tables.items()):
0442 |         for row in rows:
0443 |             fields = {key: value for key, value in row.items()
0444 |                       if key != "id" and isinstance(value, str) and value in identifiers}
0445 |             if fields:
0446 |                 result.append(_impact(decision, table, _key(row), impact,
0447 |                                       {"matched_identifier_fields": fields,
0448 |                                        "links_retained_on_original_records": True,
0449 |                                        "semantic_equivalence_asserted": False}))
0450 |     return result
0451 | 
0452 | 
0453 | def _new_text(tables, proposal, decision, old):
0454 |     _text_version(old)
0455 |     body = proposal["value"]
0456 |     version_id = _derived(proposal["id"], "text")
0457 |     analysis_id = _derived(proposal["id"], "analysis")
0458 |     document_id = _derived(proposal["id"], "unit", "document")
0459 |     original_analyses = [a["id"] for a in tables.get("analysis", []) if a.get("version_id") == old["id"]]
0460 |     old_units = [u for u in tables.get("unit", []) if u.get("version_id") == old["id"]]
0461 |     identifiers = {old["id"], *original_analyses, *(u["id"] for u in old_units)}
0462 |     impacts = _references(tables, identifiers, decision, "reanchoring_review_required")
0463 |     cache = {}
0464 |     for unit in old_units:
0465 |         a, b = unit.get("start"), unit.get("end")
0466 |         if type(a) is not int or type(b) is not int or not 0 <= a < b <= len(old["body"]):
0467 |             raise RevisionError("original unit has invalid source bounds")
0468 |         excerpt = old["body"][a:b]
0469 |         if excerpt not in cache:
0470 |             cache[excerpt] = _positions(body, excerpt)
0471 |         candidates, truncated = cache[excerpt]
0472 |         state = "missing" if not candidates else "ambiguous" if len(candidates) > 1 or truncated else "unique"
0473 |         impacts.append(_impact(decision, "unit", unit["id"], "reanchor_" + state + "_candidate",
0474 |                                {"new_version_id": version_id, "candidates": candidates,
0475 |                                 "candidates_truncated": truncated,
0476 |                                 "automatic_reattachment": False,
0477 |                                 "semantic_equivalence_asserted": False}))
0478 |     new = dict(old)
0479 |     new.update(id=version_id, body=body,
0480 |                body_sha256=hashlib.sha256(body.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
0481 |                source_path="edition/proposals/" + proposal["id"] + ".json",
0482 |                source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(_bytes(proposal)).hexdigest(),
0483 |                locator="body revision of " + old["id"],
0484 |                transcription_status="revision accepted; original source verification not inherited",
0485 |                provenance=_json({"proposal_id": proposal["id"], "decision_id": decision["id"],
0486 |                                  "previous_version_id": old["id"],
0487 |                                  "source_verification_inherited": False}))
0488 |     _text_version(new)
0489 |     _append(tables, "text_version", new)
0490 |     _append(tables, "analysis", {"id": analysis_id, "version_id": version_id,
0491 |                                 "method": "mechanical paragraphs and written tokens; semantic links require review",
0492 |                                 "scope": "entire revised body", "status": "proposed", "supersedes": None})
0493 |     units = [{"id": document_id, "analysis_id": analysis_id, "version_id": version_id,
0494 |               "kind": "document", "label": "revised text", "start": 0, "end": len(body),
0495 |               "parent_id": None, "note": "New version; no semantic equivalence or retained alignment asserted."}]
0496 |     for number, (a, b) in enumerate(_paragraphs(body), 1):
0497 |         paragraph_id = _derived(proposal["id"], "unit", f"paragraph:{a}:{b}")
0498 |         units.append({"id": paragraph_id, "analysis_id": analysis_id, "version_id": version_id,
0499 |                       "kind": "paragraph", "label": f"paragraph {number}", "start": a, "end": b,
0500 |                       "parent_id": document_id, "note": "Mechanical paragraph division; proposed analysis."})
0501 |         for word_number, (word_start, word_end) in enumerate(word_spans(body[a:b]), 1):
0502 |             x, y = a + word_start, a + word_end
0503 |             units.append({"id": _derived(proposal["id"], "unit", f"word:{x}:{y}"),
0504 |                           "analysis_id": analysis_id, "version_id": version_id, "kind": "word",
0505 |                           "label": f"p{number} word {word_number}", "start": x, "end": y,
0506 |                           "parent_id": paragraph_id, "note": "Mechanical written token; no linguistic segmentation review claimed."})
0507 |     for unit in units:
0508 |         _append(tables, "unit", unit)
0509 |     metadata = {"previous_version_id": old["id"], "new_version_id": version_id,
0510 |                 "new_analysis_id": analysis_id, "previous_analysis_ids": original_analyses,
0511 |                 "new_unit_count": len(units), "old_units_and_links_preserved": True,
0512 |                 "automatic_reattachment": False, "semantic_equivalence_asserted": False,
0513 |                 "source_verification_inherited": False}
0514 |     impacts.append(_impact(decision, "text_version", old["id"], "new_version_created", metadata))
0515 |     impacts.append(_impact(decision, "text_version", version_id, "revision_version", metadata))
0516 |     impacts.append(_impact(decision, "analysis", analysis_id, "new_proposed_analysis", metadata))
0517 |     return metadata, impacts
0518 | 
0519 | 
0520 | def _project_one(tables, proposal, decision, row):
0521 |     _value(proposal["kind"], proposal["field"], proposal["value"])
0522 |     if proposal["value"] == row[proposal["field"]]:
0523 |         raise RevisionError("proposal would make no change")
0524 |     if proposal["kind"] == "text_version":
0525 |         return _new_text(tables, proposal, decision, row)
0526 |     impacts = _references(tables, {proposal["target_id"]}, decision, "interpretation_review_required")
0527 |     # Outgoing relationships also need attention: e.g. a changed rule does not
0528 |     # silently rewrite its associated recorded decision or renderings.
0529 |     outgoing = {v for k, v in row.items() if k.endswith("_id") and isinstance(v, str)}
0530 |     for table, rows in sorted(tables.items()):
0531 |         for related in rows:
0532 |             if related.get("id") in outgoing:
0533 |                 impacts.append(_impact(decision, table, related["id"], "associated_record_review_required",
0534 |                                        {"changed_kind": proposal["kind"], "changed_id": proposal["target_id"],
0535 |                                         "not_automatically_rewritten": True}))
0536 |     row[proposal["field"]] = proposal["value"]
0537 |     return {"projection_only": True, "original_preserved_in_base_and_history": True}, impacts
0538 | 
0539 | 
0540 | def read_events(project):
0541 |     """Read and validate the immutable event journal without projecting data."""
0542 |     return _read_events(_project(project))
0543 | 
0544 | 
0545 | def apply(tables, events):
0546 |     """Purely project the complete journal, checking original preconditions.
0547 | 
0548 |     Input tables and events are not mutated. Pass the immutable base tables and
0549 |     ALL proposal/decision events exactly once, not an already projected copy.
0550 |     """
0551 |     if not isinstance(tables, dict) or any(not isinstance(rows, list) for rows in tables.values()):
0552 |         raise RevisionError("tables must map table names to row lists")
0553 |     if any(not isinstance(row, dict) for rows in tables.values() for row in rows):
0554 |         raise RevisionError("table rows must be dictionaries")
0555 |     projected = deepcopy(tables)
0556 |     proposals, decided, superseded = {}, set(), set()
0557 |     history, impacts = [], []
0558 |     for event in _ordered(events):
0559 |         _evidence(projected, event["evidence"])
0560 |         if event["event_type"] == "proposal":
0561 |             _check(projected, event, superseded)
0562 |             proposals[event["id"]] = event
0563 |             continue
0564 |         proposal_id = event["proposal_id"]
0565 |         if proposal_id not in proposals:
0566 |             raise RevisionError("decision precedes or lacks its proposal")
0567 |         if proposal_id in decided:
0568 |             raise RevisionError("proposal has more than one decision")
0569 |         decided.add(proposal_id)
0570 |         proposal = proposals[proposal_id]
0571 |         stale = False
0572 |         try:
0573 |             row = _check(projected, proposal, superseded)
0574 |         except RevisionConflict:
0575 |             if event["accept"]:
0576 |                 raise
0577 |             stale = True
0578 |             row = None
0579 |         metadata = {"proposal_by": proposal["by"], "proposal_recorded_at": proposal["recorded_at"],
0580 |                     "proposal_reason": proposal["reason"], "target_sha256": proposal["target_sha256"],
0581 |                     "stale_at_decision": stale}
0582 |         if event["accept"]:
0583 |             extra, affected = _project_one(projected, proposal, event, row)
0584 |             metadata.update(extra)
0585 |             impacts.extend(affected)
0586 |             if proposal["kind"] == "text_version":
0587 |                 superseded.add(proposal["target_id"])
0588 |         references = list(dict.fromkeys(proposal["evidence"] + event["evidence"]))
0589 |         history.append({"id": event["id"], "proposal_id": proposal_id,
0590 |                         "kind": proposal["kind"], "target_id": proposal["target_id"],
0591 |                         "field": proposal["field"], "old_value_json": _json(proposal["old_value"]),
0592 |                         "new_value_json": _json(proposal["value"]), "by_name": event["by"],
0593 |                         "reason": event["reason"], "evidence_json": _json(references),
0594 |                         "recorded_at": event["recorded_at"],
0595 |                         "status": "accepted" if event["accept"] else "rejected",
0596 |                         "metadata_json": _json(metadata)})
0597 |     # Composite keys in change_impact must remain unique even if an entity is
0598 |     # encountered through more than one exact identifier relationship.
0599 |     unique = {(r["revision_id"], r["entity_kind"], r["entity_id"], r["impact"]): r for r in impacts}
0600 |     return projected, {"revision_history": history, "change_impact": list(unique.values())}
```

0207 | WHEN NEW.status != 'draft' BEGIN
0208 |     SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'create alignment in draft status');
0209 | END;

0212 |     SELECT CASE WHEN OLD.status != 'draft' OR NEW.id != OLD.id
0213 |         OR NEW.left_version != OLD.left_version OR NEW.right_version != OLD.right_version
0214 |         OR NEW.relation != OLD.relation OR NEW.note != OLD.note
0215 |         THEN RAISE(ABORT, 'alignment identity is immutable') END;
0216 |     SELECT CASE WHEN NEW.status = 'proposed' AND (
0217 |         NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM alignment_member WHERE alignment_id = OLD.id AND side = 'left')
0218 |         OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM alignment_member WHERE alignment_id = OLD.id AND side = 'right')
0219 |     ) THEN RAISE(ABORT, 'proposed alignment requires both sides') END;

0233 | END;

0236 |     SELECT CASE WHEN (SELECT status FROM alignment WHERE id = NEW.alignment_id) != 'draft'

0239 |         SELECT CASE NEW.side WHEN 'left' THEN left_version ELSE right_version END

0250 |         THEN RAISE(ABORT, 'span exceeds text version') END;
0251 |     SELECT CASE WHEN NEW.parent_id IS NOT NULL AND NOT EXISTS (
0252 |         SELECT 1 FROM unit p WHERE p.id = NEW.parent_id
0253 |           AND p.analysis_id = NEW.analysis_id AND p.version_id = NEW.version_id
0254 |           AND p.start <= NEW.start AND p.end >= NEW.end
0255 |     ) THEN RAISE(ABORT, 'parent must already exist, contain child and share analysis/version') END;

0090 | class DatabaseTests(unittest.TestCase):
0091 |     def fresh(self, version=None):
0092 |         db = database.connect()
0093 |         self.addCleanup(db.close)
0094 |         database.migrate(db, target=version)
0095 |         return db
0096 | 
0097 |     def populated(self):
0098 |         db = self.fresh()
0099 |         database.insert_records(db, fixture())
0100 |         return db
0101 | 
0102 |     def test_connection_and_packaged_v1_upgrade_preserve_schema_and_rows(self):
0103 |         db = self.fresh(1)
0104 |         self.assertIsInstance(db.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone(), sqlite3.Row)
0105 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys").fetchone()[0], 1)
0106 |         before = dict(db.execute("SELECT name, sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE sql IS NOT NULL AND name != 'schema_migration'"))
0107 |         base_tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")} - {"schema_migration"}
0108 |         self.assertEqual(len(base_tables), 33)
0109 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='trigger'").fetchone()[0], 12)
0110 |         database.insert_records(db, {"decision": [dict(id="old", question="q", resolution="r", authority="test", status="provisional")]})
0111 |         self.assertEqual(database.migrate(db), 2)
0112 |         for name, sql in before.items():
0113 |             self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()[0], sql)
0114 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT resolution FROM decision WHERE id='old'").fetchone()[0], "r")
0115 |         self.assertEqual(database.migrate(db), 2)
0116 |         history = list(db.execute("SELECT version, name, sha256 FROM schema_migration ORDER BY version"))
0117 |         for row in history:
0118 |             path = Path(database.__file__).with_name("migrations") / row[1]
0119 |             self.assertEqual(row[2], hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
0120 | 
0121 |     def test_future_version_checksum_and_schema_drift_fail_before_mutation(self):
0122 |         with self.subTest("future version"):
0123 |             db = self.fresh(1)
0124 |             db.execute("PRAGMA user_version=999")
0125 |             with self.assertRaisesRegex(database.SchemaError, "future"):
0126 |                 database.migrate(db)
0127 |         with self.subTest("future target"):
0128 |             with self.assertRaisesRegex(database.SchemaError, "unknown target"):
0129 |                 database.migrate(self.fresh(), target=999)
0130 |         with self.subTest("checksum"):
0131 |             db = self.fresh(1)
0132 |             db.execute("UPDATE schema_migration SET sha256=?", ("0" * 64,))
0133 |             with self.assertRaisesRegex(database.SchemaError, "checksum"):
0134 |                 database.migrate(db)
0135 |             self.assertEqual(db.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0], 1)
0136 |         for sql in ("CREATE TABLE unexpected(id TEXT)", "ALTER TABLE decision ADD COLUMN unexpected TEXT",
0137 |                     "DROP TRIGGER unit_bounds", "DROP VIEW unit_text"):
0138 |             with self.subTest(sql):
0139 |                 db = self.fresh(1)
0140 |                 db.execute(sql)
0141 |                 with self.assertRaises(database.SchemaError):
0142 |                     database.migrate(db)
0143 |                 self.assertEqual(db.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0], 1)
0144 | 
0145 |     def test_unversioned_nonempty_database_and_downgrade_are_rejected(self):
0146 |         db = database.connect()
0147 |         self.addCleanup(db.close)
0148 |         db.execute("CREATE TABLE unknown(id TEXT)")
0149 |         with self.assertRaisesRegex(database.SchemaError, "unversioned"):
0150 |             database.migrate(db)
0151 |         with self.assertRaisesRegex(database.SchemaError, "downgrade"):
0152 |             database.migrate(self.fresh(), target=1)
0153 | 
0154 |     def test_failed_upgrade_rolls_back_ddl_history_and_user_version(self):
0155 |         db = self.fresh(1)
0156 |         migrations = database._migrations()
0157 |         broken = "CREATE TABLE partial(id TEXT);\nINVALID SQL;\n"
0158 |         altered = (migrations[0], (2, migrations[1][1], broken, hashlib.sha256(broken.encode()).hexdigest()))
0159 |         with mock.patch.object(database, "_migrations", return_value=altered):
0160 |             with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.OperationalError):
0161 |                 database.migrate(db)
0162 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0], 1)
0163 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM schema_migration").fetchone()[0], 1)
0164 |         self.assertIsNone(db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='partial'").fetchone())
0165 |         self.assertEqual(database.migrate(db), 2)
0166 | 
0167 |     def test_import_orders_parents_deferred_analysis_and_alignment_freeze(self):
0168 |         records = fixture()
0169 |         original = copy.deepcopy(records)
0170 |         db = self.fresh()
0171 |         database.insert_records(db, records)
0172 |         self.assertEqual(records, original)
0173 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT text FROM unit_text WHERE id='a-child'").fetchone()[0], "beta")
0174 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT status FROM alignment WHERE id='a'").fetchone()[0], "proposed")
0175 |         self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT supersedes FROM analysis WHERE id='g1'").fetchone()[0], "g0")
0176 |         self.assertEqual(database.report(db)["foreign_key_errors"], [])
0177 |         with self.assertRaisesRegex(sqlite3.IntegrityError, "frozen"):
0178 |             db.execute("DELETE FROM alignment_member WHERE alignment_id='a'")

0059 | 
0060 | def proposal(tables, sequence=1, *, kind="term_sense", target="fixed-sense", field="rule", value="new rule"):
0061 |     row = next(r for r in tables[kind] if r["id"] == target)
0062 |     return dict(format=rev.FORMAT, schema_version=1, event_type="proposal", id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
0063 |                 sequence=sequence, recorded_at=f"2026-01-01T00:00:00.{sequence:06d}Z",
0064 |                 by="synthetic proposer", reason="synthetic reason", evidence=["ev-opaque"],
0065 |                 kind=kind, target_id=target, field=field, value=value,
0066 |                 old_value=row[field], target_sha256=rev.fingerprint(row))
0067 | 
0068 | 
0069 | def decision(p, sequence=2, *, accept=True):
0070 |     return dict(format=rev.FORMAT, schema_version=1, event_type="decision", id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
0071 |                 sequence=sequence, recorded_at=f"2026-01-01T00:00:00.{sequence:06d}Z",
0072 |                 by="synthetic decider", reason="explicit synthetic decision", evidence=list(p["evidence"]),
0073 |                 proposal_id=p["id"], accept=accept)
0074 |

### FILE: docs/architecture/user-guide.md
```text
0001 | # Build, inspect and revise the edition
0002 | 
0003 | The current engine uses Python 3.11+ and its standard library. No model credentials, database server or package installation is needed. Run commands from the project root; `python3 humboldt` is equivalent to `./humboldt`. An installed package can instead expose the `humboldt` command through `pyproject.toml`.
0004 | 
0005 | ## Start reading
0006 | 
0007 | ```bash
0008 | ./humboldt validate
0009 | ./humboldt build
0010 | ./humboldt browse
0011 | ```
0012 | 
0013 | Open the loopback URL printed by `browse`. Choose §1, §2 or §147 and a text version. Click a word or block to inspect its ancestry, overlapping annotations, evidence, translation alternatives and decisions. Labels distinguish exact selected spans from contextual associations; a sentence alignment is not presented as word-for-word equivalence. Search accepts German and English, including saved terminology renderings. The reader makes no database changes.
0014 | 
0015 | The manifest’s `reading_defaults` selects the checked source and revised English for each prepared section. Every version remains selectable, labelled with its permanent ID. These are reading defaults, separate from editorial acceptance. To make a later successor the default, update that explicit manifest entry and rebuild.
0016 | 
0017 | ```bash
0018 | ./humboldt search understanding
0019 | ./humboldt passage 147
0020 | ./humboldt trace 'difficult-147-de-v1:s05:w013'
0021 | ./humboldt show term_sense 'lex:verstand:s01'
0022 | ./humboldt status
0023 | ```
0024 | 
0025 | Checked text and evidence are embedded in the database. Original source attachments may be absent in a portable copy; the reader says so. `./humboldt validate --sources` additionally checks hashes of attachments available in this project. Missing originals do not upgrade or invalidate the recorded quotation-check status. The unavailable critical edition and specialist-validation limits remain as before.
0026 | 
0027 | ## Propose and review a correction
0028 | 
0029 | An entity page offers a proposal form for supported fields: text-version body; terminology definition, rule or caution; and decision resolution. Supply attribution, reason and existing evidence IDs where applicable, then download the draft. This saves no editorial change. Import it into the working project:
0030 | 
0031 | ```bash
0032 | ./humboldt import-proposal /path/to/humboldt-proposal-draft.json
0033 | ./humboldt history
0034 | ./humboldt decide PROPOSAL_UUID --accept --by 'Editor name' --reason 'Evidence-based explanation'
0035 | ./humboldt build --force
0036 | ```
0037 | 
0038 | Use `--reject` for an explicit rejection. The downloaded target fingerprint must still match; stale acceptance fails and needs a new proposal. Rejection remains recordable after another change. The CLI also supports direct `propose`; see `./humboldt propose --help`. `--value-file` reads the exact replacement text from a UTF-8 file.
0039 | 
0040 | Accepted interpretation changes create a current projection while base values and attributed history remain preserved. Related decisions or uses are flagged for review rather than rewritten automatically. A text correction creates a new text version and a new proposed mechanical analysis. Old readings and alignments remain retrievable. Reattachment candidates are labelled exact, ambiguous or missing; they are not silently accepted as semantic correspondences. To revise a successor text, target its new permanent ID explicitly. Alternative witnesses/parallel translations can also be added as independent versions in a new dataset.
0041 | 
0042 | Authoring appends canonical, ordered JSON events under `edition/proposals/` and `edition/changes/`. An exclusive project lock prevents cooperating commands from losing changes. A leftover lock requires inspection; the engine never deletes it automatically. Attribution is a recorded claim, not authenticated human expertise. No German-fidelity approval is inferred from an accepted software operation.
0043 | 
0044 | ## Add another passage as data
0045 | 
0046 | Use the schema in `src/humboldt/record_schemas/bundle.schema.json` and the explicitly synthetic template `tests/fixtures/minimal/edition/passages/minimal.json` (packaged as `examples/minimal-passage.json` in the release). Supply `format: humboldt.passage`, `schema_version: 1`, a new dataset ID, logical `section`, text-version records, layouts and any evidence/decisions/alignments. IDs are permanent literals; generating another passage must not renumber existing senses. Text hashes cover exact UTF-8 content. Token positions count Unicode code points in that immutable text version.
0047 | 
0048 | ```bash
0049 | ./humboldt import /path/to/new-passage.json
0050 | ./humboldt build --force
0051 | ```
0052 | 
0053 | The importer validates the complete candidate edition before publishing its manifest entry. Paragraph/sentence/rendering and written-token units are generated from layouts. Consequential annotations and many-to-many correspondences are declared as records. No new passage-specific Python builder is needed. This command adds prepared records; it does not translate, infer a parse or invent evidence.
0054 | 
0055 | ## Export and release
0056 | 
0057 | ```bash
0058 | ./humboldt export --output build/edition.json
0059 | ./humboldt export --format csv --table term_sense --output build/senses.csv
0060 | ./humboldt release --output releases/0.1.0
0061 | ```
0062 | 
0063 | The release contains the runnable engine, durable inputs, current sealed database, guide and file-hash manifest. Packaging validates the copy in a fresh Python subprocess. It does not publish anything. A recipient can run `python3 humboldt validate`, rebuild into `build/`, or browse the included snapshot without the original `tmp/`, legacy scripts, source PDFs or model sessions. Edit a working copy; retain the original release for citation and comparison.
0064 | 
0065 | `build/` is disposable output; removing it never removes authoritative inputs. Release destinations and export files must be fresh. `build --force` is confined to project `build/`; it cannot replace source records or historical snapshots. Sealed/read-only SQLite connections prevent accidental edits through normal readers. A database owner can remove SQLite protections, so the durable records, journal and release hashes are the reproducibility evidence.
0066 | 
0067 | ## Tests and compatibility
0068 | 
0069 | ```bash
0070 | ./humboldt test
0071 | ./humboldt test --legacy
0072 | ```
0073 | 
0074 | The default suite uses small synthetic fixtures and clean portable copies. Legacy tests additionally need the original holdings and old snapshots; they preserve the earlier research workflow. Database migrations are packaged, checksummed and tested on small old schemas; unknown future versions and changed migration history fail explicitly. Sealed releases are rebuilt rather than upgraded in place. JSON format changes require a separately versioned converter; no arbitrary future format is silently accepted.
0075 | 
0076 | The software preserves source-grounding distinctions. Successful builds, schema checks and interface tests establish computational properties, not German fidelity or the truth of historical linguistic claims.
```


### FILE: docs/architecture/data-contract.md
```text
0001 | # Data and computation contract
0002 | 
0003 | ## Authority and generation
0004 | 
0005 | `edition/manifest.json` names and hashes the durable datasets. Four initial bundles preserve the existing scholarly database: text/layout records, evidence/decisions, terminology, and the §147 passage. Canonical record IDs are explicit immutable identities. Old list-derived names are retained as fixed legacy identifiers; new code never recomputes sense identity from list order. Derived word IDs are scoped to immutable text versions.
0006 | 
0007 | The database and reading views are projections of those datasets plus the append-only proposal/decision journal. They are not the authoritative editing surface. Original sources and historical model-run artifacts remain separately preserved; rebuilding the edition never invokes a model, a historical builder or an ignored database. The one-time `tools/migrate_legacy_snapshot.py` is migration evidence, not a normal dependency.
0008 | 
0009 | ## Generic passage apparatus
0010 | 
0011 | A passage declares its logical section and versions. Layouts specify stable paragraph and segment IDs and exact source-sentence/rendering block text. Shared code generates written-token units using `unicode_lmn_v1`: Unicode letters/marks/numbers, retaining internal hyphens and apostrophes. It does not claim linguistic segmentation. Explicit annotation records can overlap and many-to-many alignments can connect multiple units on either side.
0012 | 
0013 | The initial canonical export stores deterministic word recipes instead of thousands of copied derived word rows. The importer verifies their expected counts and reconstructs the exact original spans, IDs, labels and discovery metadata. All 33 pre-existing scholarly tables were compared row-for-row after the first reconstruction; provenance records store counts and logical table hashes.
0014 | 
0015 | ## Versions and change
0016 | 
0017 | Record schemas and SQL schemas have distinct versions. SQL migrations are bundled with the engine and checked against their history; they evolve storage without licensing a change of meaning. Canonical input hashes, body hashes, parent/span checks, foreign keys and exact anchoring apply at build time. `input_digest` identifies the manifest and journal; `logical_digest` identifies durable logical table content independent of build time, path and SQLite file layout.
0018 | 
0019 | Accepted interpretation changes preserve before/after values and attributed decisions. Text revisions create new versions and proposed analyses, with explicit reattachment/impact records. Old alignments remain attached to their original versions. The engine does not infer that a copied span carries the same meaning after revision. The local reader can inspect old and current records and download fingerprinted proposals; importing or accepting one is a separate authoring action.
0020 | 
0021 | ## Publication and recovery
0022 | 
0023 | Builds publish a complete, validated database atomically; normal fresh outputs cannot overwrite another file. Authoring serializes cooperating writers with a project lock and publishes one immutable event per file. Imports validate before switching the manifest. An interruption may leave an unreferenced candidate file or lock for inspection, not an intentionally half-validated active manifest. File and directory syncing improves crash durability on supporting filesystems; filesystem guarantees remain relevant.
0024 | 
0025 | Optional source attachments are declared resources. Their absence is reported, while embedded transcriptions/quotations retain their recorded verification level. The preserved 1906 provisional-base policy, uncertain source readings and lack of specialist validation remain visible in the actual data.
0026 | 
0027 | Generated `build/` and local `releases/` are excluded from Git. Source code, schema resources, durable edition records, tests and maintained documentation are the material intended for version control. The repository remains local and uncommitted until a separate commit/publication instruction.
```
